  "Trainer":            {icon:"🎯", sub:"ট্রেনিং ও পারফরম্যান্স প্যানেল", hi:"আজকের টার্গেট পূরণ করার সময়!"},
  "Senior Team Leader": {icon:"👑", sub:"প্রিমিয়াম লিডারশিপ প্যানেল", hi:"প্রিমিয়াম লিডারশিপ প্যানেলে স্বাগতম"},

const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const today = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
function toast(m, err){ const t=$('toast'); t.textContent=m; t.className='show'+(err?' err':''); clearTimeout(toast.t); toast.t=setTimeout(()=>t.className='',2800); }
function lsGet(k,def){ try{ return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)) ?? def; }catch(e){ return def; } }

/* ===== ব্যাকগ্রাউন্ড অ্যানিমেশন (ভিডিওর মতো লাইভ ইফেক্ট) ===== */
const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.id = 'fx'; document.body.prepend(cv);
const cx = cv.getContext('2d'); let P = [], fxMode = null, W = 0, H = 0;
const FXM = {"Team Leader":'orb',"Trainer":'line',"Teacher":'star',"Senior Team Leader":'dust'};
function rs(){ W = cv.width = innerWidth; H = cv.height = innerHeight; }
addEventListener('resize', rs); rs();
function mk(r){ return {x:Math.random()*W, y:r?Math.random()*H:(fxMode==='line'?-20:H+20), s:Math.random()*2+1, v:Math.random()*.6+.25, p:Math.random()*6.28, c:Math.random()}; }
function fxInit(cat){ const m = FXM[cat] || 'orb'; if(m === fxMode) return; fxMode = m; P = Array.from({length: m==='orb'?50:m==='line'?38:45}, () => mk(true)); }
function fxLoop(t){
  cx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
  if(fxMode==='orb'){
    P.forEach(p => { p.y -= p.v; p.x += Math.sin(t/1500+p.p)*.3; if(p.y < -20) Object.assign(p, mk(false));
      cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(26,188,156,.45)'; cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(p.x,p.y,p.s*2.2,0,6.28); cx.fill(); });
    for(let i=0;i<P.length;i++) for(let j=i+1;j<P.length;j++){
      const dx=P[i].x-P[j].x, dy=P[i].y-P[j].y, d=dx*dx+dy*dy;
      if(d<10000){ cx.strokeStyle = `rgba(26,188,156,${.18*(1-d/10000)})`; cx.beginPath(); cx.moveTo(P[i].x,P[i].y); cx.lineTo(P[j].x,P[j].y); cx.stroke(); } }
  } else if(fxMode==='line'){
    cx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    P.forEach(p => { p.x += p.v*3; p.y += p.v*3; if(p.x > W || p.y > H){ p.x = Math.random()*W - 200; p.y = -20; }
      cx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56,189,248,.4)'; cx.beginPath(); cx.moveTo(p.x,p.y); cx.lineTo(p.x-30*p.s, p.y-30*p.s); cx.stroke(); });
  } else if(fxMode==='star'){
    const col = ['#ec4899','#7c3aed','#fbbf24'];
    P.forEach(p => { p.y -= p.v*.6; p.x += Math.sin(t/1200+p.p)*.4; if(p.y < -20) Object.assign(p, mk(false));
      cx.save(); cx.globalAlpha = .5; cx.fillStyle = col[Math.floor(p.c*3)]; cx.font = (p.s*7+6)+'px sans-serif';
      cx.translate(p.x,p.y); cx.rotate(t/1500+p.p); cx.fillText('✦',0,0); cx.restore(); });
  } else if(fxMode==='dust'){
    P.forEach(p => { p.y -= p.v*.35; p.x += Math.sin(t/2000+p.p)*.2; if(p.y < -10) Object.assign(p, mk(false));
      cx.fillStyle = `rgba(245,215,110,${.2+.6*Math.abs(Math.sin(t/700+p.p))})`; cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(p.x,p.y,p.s*1.2,0,6.28); cx.fill(); });
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(fxLoop);
}
if(!matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) requestAnimationFrame(fxLoop);

/* ===== সংখ্যা কাউন্ট-আপ অ্যানিমেশন (ডাটা অপরিবর্তিত) ===== */
function countUp(id){
  const el = $(id); el._n = 0; el._last = el.textContent;
  new MutationObserver(() => {
    if(el.textContent === el._last) return;
    const fin = el.textContent, m = fin.match(/^(\D*)([\d.,]+)(.*)$/); el._last = fin;
    if(!m) return; const to = parseFloat(m[2].replace(/,/g,'')); if(isNaN(to)) return;
    const from = el._n, t0 = performance.now(); cancelAnimationFrame(el._raf);
    const step = now => {
      const k = Math.min(1,(now-t0)/900), v = from+(to-from)*(1-Math.pow(1-k,3));
      const txt = k<1 ? m[1]+Math.round(v)+m[3] : fin; el._last = txt; el.textContent = txt;
      if(k<1) el._raf = requestAnimationFrame(step); else el._n = to;
    };
    el._raf = requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }).observe(el,{childList:true,characterData:true,subtree:true});
}

/* ===== রিপল + কনফেটি ===== */
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('.btn-primary,.cat-tab,.dash-box,#nav button,.quick button,.back-btn'); if(!b) return;
  const r = b.getBoundingClientRect(), s = document.createElement('span'), d = Math.max(r.width,r.height);
  s.className = 'rip'; s.style.cssText = `width:${d}px;height:${d}px;left:${e.clientX-r.left-d/2}px;top:${e.clientY-r.top-d/2}px`;
  b.appendChild(s); setTimeout(() => s.remove(), 600);
});
function boom(){
  const E = ['🎉','✨','⭐','💎','🎊','💚'];
  for(let i=0;i<28;i++){ const s = document.createElement('span'); s.className = 'cf'; s.textContent = E[i%E.length];
    s.style.cssText = `left:${Math.random()*100}vw;font-size:${14+Math.random()*16}px;animation-duration:${1.6+Math.random()*1.4}s;animation-delay:${Math.random()*.4}s`;
    document.body.appendChild(s); setTimeout(() => s.remove(), 3600); }
}

function setTheme(cat){
  if(!THEMES[cat]) cat = "Team Leader";
  selectedCat = cat;
  document.body.setAttribute('data-theme', cat);
  $('lIcon').innerText = THEMES[cat].icon;
  $('lTitle').innerText = cat + " Login";
  $('lSub').innerText = THEMES[cat].sub;
  $('loginBtn').innerText = THEMES[cat].icon + " Login as " + cat;
  document.querySelectorAll('.cat-tab').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.cat === cat));
  fxInit(cat);
}
function buildTabs(){
  $('catTabs').innerHTML = Object.keys(THEMES).map(c =>
    `<button class="cat-tab" data-cat="${c}" onclick="setTheme('${c}')">${THEMES[c].icon} ${c}</button>`).join('');
}
function togglePw(){ const i=$('loginPassword'); i.type = i.type==='password' ? 'text' : 'password'; }
function greet(){ const h = +new Date().toLocaleString('en-US',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka',hour:'numeric',hour12:false})%24;
  return h<5?'শুভ রাত্রি':h<12?'শুভ সকাল':h<16?'শুভ দুপুর':h<18?'শুভ বিকাল':'শুভ সন্ধ্যা'; }

/* ===== প্রতি ক্যাটাগরির আলাদা ফিচার ===== */
function ring(pct){ return `<div class="ring"><svg viewBox="0 0 36 36"><path class="rb" d="M18 2a16 16 0 1 1 0 32a16 16 0 1 1 0-32"/><path class="rf" stroke-dasharray="${Math.round(pct*1.005)},100" d="M18 2a16 16 0 1 1 0 32a16 16 0 1 1 0-32"/></svg><b>${pct}%</b></div>`; }
function bars(a){ const D=['S','M','T','W','T','F','S']; return `<div class="bars">${a.map((h,i)=>`<i style="height:${h}%"><em>${D[i]}</em></i>`).join('')}</div>`; }
function goal(n){ const g = Math.max(10, Math.ceil((n+1)/10)*10); return {g, pct: Math.min(100, Math.round(n/g*100))}; }
const TASKS = {
  "Trainer": ["নতুন একজন স্টুডেন্টকে ট্রেনিং দিন","গতকালের ফলো-আপ সম্পন্ন করুন","একটি টিপস টিমের সাথে শেয়ার করুন"],
};
function toggleTask(i){ const k='tasks_'+today(); const a=lsGet(k,[]); const x=a.indexOf(i); x<0?a.push(i):a.splice(x,1); localStorage.setItem(k,JSON.stringify(a)); renderExtras(); }
function setMood(m){ localStorage.setItem('mood_'+today(), m); renderExtras(); }
function renderExtras(){
  const d = currentSubAdminData; if(!d) return;
  if(document.activeElement && document.activeElement.classList.contains('note')) return;
  const n = globalStudentsCache.length, G = goal(n), nm = esc(d.name), id = esc(d.loginId);
  const done = lsGet('tasks_'+today(), []), mood = localStorage.getItem('mood_'+today());
  const T = {
    "Trainer": `
      <div class="x-card">${ring(G.pct)}<div><h4>🎯 Student Goal</h4><p>${n}/${G.g} স্টুডেন্ট — আর ${G.g-n} জন হলেই পরের মাইলফলক!</p></div></div>

      <div class="x-card"><div><h4>💬 আজকের অনুপ্রেরণা</h4><p>নিয়মিত অনুশীলনই সাফল্যের চাবিকাঠি।</p></div></div>
      <div class="chips"><span>🏅 Active Trainer</span><span>⚡ XP ${n*50}</span><span>🔥 Rank ${n>=25?'Pro':n>=10?'Skilled':'Rookie'}</span></div>`,
    "Teacher": `
      <div class="x-card col" style="background:var(--hero);position:relative;overflow:hidden;min-height:90px"><span class="fl">🌸</span><span class="fl f2">⭐</span><h4 style="color:#fff">${greet()}, ${nm}! 🎒</h4><p style="color:#fff">আপনার ${n} জন শিক্ষার্থী আপনার দিকে তাকিয়ে আছে</p></div>
      <div class="x-card col"><h4>😊 আজ আপনার মেজাজ কেমন?</h4><div class="moods">${['😄','🙂','😴','🤩'].map(m=>`<button class="${mood===m?'on':''}" onclick="setMood('${m}')">${m}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
      <div class="x-card">${ring(G.pct)}<div><h4>🌱 Student Garden</h4><p>${n} জন শিক্ষার্থী — পরের লক্ষ্য ${G.g} জন</p></div></div>
      <div class="x-card col"><h4>📝 আজকের লেসন নোট</h4><textarea class="note" oninput="localStorage.setItem('note_'+today(),this.value)" placeholder="আজকের পরিকল্পনা লিখুন...">${esc(localStorage.getItem('note_'+today())||'')}</textarea></div>
      <div class="x-card col"><h4>⭐ Teacher Badges</h4><div class="trophies">${[[1,'🍎','প্রথম'],[10,'📖','১০ জন'],[25,'💖','২৫ জন'],[50,'🏫','৫০ জন']].map(b=>`<div class="${n>=b[0]?'':'lock'}">${b[1]}<small>${b[2]}</small></div>`).join('')}</div></div>
      <div class="chips"><span>🍎 Best Teacher</span><span>💖 Loved by Students</span><span>📖 Smart Mentor</span></div>`,
    "Team Leader": `

      <div class="x-card col"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center"><h4>📡 Team Pulse</h4><span class="live">LIVE</span></div><svg class="hb" viewBox="0 0 200 40" preserveAspectRatio="none"><path class="base" d="M0 20h60l8-16 10 32 8-16h114"/><path d="M0 20h60l8-16 10 32 8-16h114"/></svg></div>
      <div class="x-card col"><h4>🏆 Level ${Math.floor(n/5)+1} — Team Leader</h4><div class="lb"><div class="pb"><i style="width:${(n%5)*20}%"></i></div><b>${(n%5)*20}%</b></div><p>পরের লেভেলের জন্য আরও ${5-(n%5)} জন স্টুডেন্ট দরকার</p></div>
      <div class="x-card">${ring(G.pct)}<div><h4>🎯 Team Target</h4><p>${n}/${G.g} স্টুডেন্ট অর্জিত</p></div></div>
      <div class="ticker"><span>📢 নতুন টিম টার্গেট চালু হয়েছে — সবাইকে শুভকামনা! &nbsp;&nbsp;🚀 আজ টিমের পারফরম্যান্স বাড়ছে!</span></div>
      <div class="chips"><span>🚀 Rising Leader</span><span>🤝 Team Builder</span><span>💼 ID: ${id}</span></div>`,

      <div class="x-card"><div class="coin">👑</div><div><h4>💎 Elite Perks</h4><p>প্রায়োরিটি সাপোর্ট • এক্সক্লুসিভ রিওয়ার্ড • VIP ব্যাজ</p></div></div>
      <div class="x-card col"><h4>🏆 Trophy Shelf</h4><div class="trophies">${[[1,'🥇','১'],[10,'💎','১০'],[25,'🏆','২৫'],[50,'⭐','৫০'],[100,'👑','১০০']].map(b=>`<div class="${n>=b[0]?'':'lock'}">${b[1]}<small>${b[2]}</small></div>`).join('')}</div></div>
      <div class="x-card">${ring(G.pct)}<div><h4>💎 Elite Progress</h4><p>${n}/${G.g} — পরের ট্রফির পথে</p></div></div>
      <div class="x-card col"><h4>📊 Leadership Growth</h4>${bars([35,50,62,70,82,90,98])}</div>
      <div class="ticker"><span>👑 VIP নোটিশ: আপনার প্রিমিয়াম মেম্বারশিপ সক্রিয় আছে &nbsp;&nbsp;💎 এক্সক্লুসিভ রিওয়ার্ড শীঘ্রই আসছে!</span></div>

  if(!renderExtras.d){ renderExtras.d = 1; ex.classList.add('first');
    [...ex.children].forEach((c,i) => c.style.animationDelay = (.15+i*.1)+'s');

window.onload = function(){
  buildTabs();
  setTheme(localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat') || "Team Leader");
  $('inIc').innerText = THEMES[selectedCat].icon;
  setTimeout(() => { $('intro').classList.add('out'); setTimeout(() => $('intro').remove(), 800); }, 2300);
  ['homeBalance','homeTotalStudents','bAvailable'].forEach(countUp);
  [...$('homeSection').children].forEach((c,i) => c.style.animationDelay = (i*.08)+'s');
  if(currentSubAdminKey) loadDashboardData(currentSubAdminKey); else showLoginView();
  ['loginId','loginPassword'].forEach(i => $(i).addEventListener('keydown', e => { if(e.key==='Enter') subAdminLogin(); }));
  setInterval(() => {
    const n = new Date();
    $('liveTime').innerText = n.toLocaleTimeString('en-US',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'});
    $('liveDate').innerText = n.toLocaleDateString('bn-BD',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka',month:'long',day:'numeric',weekday:'long'});
  },1000);
};

function showLoginView(){ $('loginView').style.display='flex'; $('dashboardView').style.display='none'; $('nav').style.display='none'; }
function showDashboardView(){ $('loginView').style.display='none'; $('dashboardView').style.display='block'; $('nav').style.display='flex'; }

function subAdminLogin(){
  const category = selectedCat;
  const loginId = $('loginId').value.trim();
  const password = $('loginPassword').value.trim();
  if(!loginId || !password){ toast("❌ Login ID ও Password দিন", true); return; }
  $('loadingOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
  const stop = () => $('loadingOverlay').style.display = 'none';
  db.ref('subAdmins').orderByChild('loginId').equalTo(loginId).once('value', snap => {
    let found = false, suspended = false, key = null;
    snap.forEach(c => {
      const sa = c.val();
      if(sa.category === category && sa.password === password){
        if(sa.status === 'Suspended') suspended = true; else { found = true; key = c.key; }
      }
    });
    if(suspended){ stop(); toast("❌ আপনার একাউন্ট সাসপেন্ড করা আছে", true); return; }
    if(!found){ stop(); toast("❌ ক্যাটাগরি, ID বা Password ভুল", true); return; }
    localStorage.setItem('subAdminKey', key);
    localStorage.setItem('subAdminCat', category);
    currentSubAdminKey = key;
    setTimeout(() => { stop(); loadDashboardData(key); }, 400);
  }).catch(err => { stop(); toast("❌ Connection Error: " + err.message, true); });
}

function loadDashboardData(key){
  showDashboardView();
  switchSubTab('home', true);

  db.ref('subAdmins/'+key).on('value', snap => {
    if(!snap.exists()){ logout(); return; }
    const d = currentSubAdminData = snap.val();
    setTheme(d.category);
    localStorage.setItem('subAdminCat', d.category);
    const t = THEMES[d.category] || THEMES["Team Leader"];
    $('topIcon').innerText = t.icon;
    $('helloIc').innerText = t.icon;
    $('helloTxt').innerText = greet() + ", " + d.name;
    $('helloSub').innerText = t.hi;
    $('topName').innerText = d.name;
    $('topCat').innerText = d.category;
    $('pName').innerText = d.name;
    $('pHeroName').innerText = d.name;
    $('pAvatar').innerText = (d.name || '?').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase();
    $('pBadge').innerText = t.icon + " " + d.category;
    $('pLoginId').innerText = d.loginId;
    $('pCategory').innerText = d.category;
    $('pBalance').innerText = "৳" + (d.balance||0);
    $('pCreated').innerText = d.createdDate || 'N/A';
    $('homeBalance').innerText = "৳ " + (d.balance||0);
    $('bAvailable').innerText = "৳" + (d.balance||0);
    $('wcName').innerText = d.name;
    $('wcId').innerText = d.loginId;
    $('bAdded').innerText = "৳" + (d.totalAdded||0);
    $('bDeducted').innerText = "৳" + (d.totalDeducted||0);
    $('bWithdrawn').innerText = "৳" + (d.totalWithdrawn||0);
    renderExtras();
  });

  db.ref('subAdmins/'+key+'/students').on('value', snap => {
    globalStudentsCache = [];
    snap.forEach(c => { const st = c.val(); st.key = c.key; globalStudentsCache.push(st); });
    $('homeTotalStudents').innerText = globalStudentsCache.length;
    $('stCount').innerText = globalStudentsCache.length;
    $('pStudents').innerText = globalStudentsCache.length;
    renderStudents();
    renderExtras();
  });

  db.ref('subAdminTransactions').orderByChild('subAdminKey').equalTo(key).on('value', snap => {
    const box = $('transactionHistoryContainer');
    if(!snap.exists()){ box.innerHTML = "<p class='empty'>এখনো কোনো লেনদেন নেই।</p>"; return; }
    const list = []; snap.forEach(c => list.push(c.val()));
    list.sort((a,b) => (b.timestamp||0)-(a.timestamp||0));
    box.innerHTML = list.map(t => `<div class="hist"><div class="hi">${/withdraw/i.test(t.type||'')?'💸':'💰'}</div><div><b>${esc(t.type||'Transaction')}</b>: ৳${esc(t.amount)}<br><span>${esc(t.description)} | ${esc(t.date||'N/A')}</span></div></div>`).join('');
  });

  db.ref('subAdminWithdrawals').orderByChild('subAdminKey').equalTo(key).on('value', snap => {
    const box = $('separateWithdrawalHistoryContainer');
    if(!snap.exists()){ box.innerHTML = "<p class='empty'>এখনো কোনো উইথড্র রিকোয়েস্ট নেই।</p>"; return; }
    const list = []; snap.forEach(c => list.push(c.val()));
    list.sort((a,b) => {
      const ap = a.status==='Pending'?1:0, bp = b.status==='Pending'?1:0;
      return ap!==bp ? bp-ap : (b.requestedAt||0)-(a.requestedAt||0);
    });
    box.innerHTML = list.map(w => {
      const ic = w.status==='Pending'?'🟡':(w.status==='Approved'?'✅':'❌');
      return `<div class="hist"><div class="hi">${ic}</div><div>৳${esc(w.amount)} via <b>${esc(w.paymentMethod)}</b> (${esc(w.paymentNumber)})<br><span>Status: <b>${esc(w.status)}</b> | ${esc(w.date||'N/A')}</span></div></div>`;
    }).join('');
  });
}

function switchSubTab(tab, noPush){
  document.querySelectorAll('.section').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
  $('homeSection').classList.toggle('hide', tab !== 'home');
  if(tab !== 'home') $(tab + 'Section').classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('#nav button').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.t === tab));
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
  if(!noPush) history.pushState({tab}, "", "#" + tab);
}
window.addEventListener('popstate', e => {
  if(!currentSubAdminKey) return;
  switchSubTab((e.state && e.state.tab) || 'home', true);
});

function copyId(){
  const v = currentSubAdminData && currentSubAdminData.loginId; if(!v) return;
  (navigator.clipboard ? navigator.clipboard.writeText(v) : Promise.reject()).then(() => toast("✅ ID কপি হয়েছে"), () => toast("❌ কপি করা যায়নি", true));
}
function setAmt(p){ if(currentSubAdminData) $('wAmount').value = Math.floor(parseFloat(currentSubAdminData.balance||0)*p/100); }

function saveStudent(){
  const name = $('sName').value.trim(), studentId = $('sId').value.trim(),
        mobile = $('sMobile').value.trim(), resellerId = $('sReseller').value.trim();
  if(!name || !studentId || !mobile || !resellerId){ toast("❌ সবগুলো ঘর পূরণ করুন", true); return; }
  db.ref('subAdmins/'+currentSubAdminKey+'/students').push({name, studentId, mobile, resellerId, createdAt: Date.now()}, err => {
    if(!err){
      toast("✅ Student Saved Successfully");
      ['sName','sId','sMobile','sReseller'].forEach(i => $(i).value = '');
      boom();
    } else toast("❌ " + err.message, true);
  });
}

function renderStudents(){
  const q = $('studentSearch').value.toLowerCase();
  const f = globalStudentsCache.filter(s =>
    (s.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (s.studentId||'').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    (s.mobile||'').includes(q) || (s.resellerId||'').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  $('studentsContainer').innerHTML = f.length ? f.map(s => `
    <div class="student-card">
      <div class="av">${esc((s.name||'?').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase())}</div>
      <div>
        <h4>${esc(s.name)}</h4>
        <p><b>ID:</b> ${esc(s.studentId)}</p>
        <p><b>Mobile:</b> ${esc(s.mobile)}</p>
        <p><b>Reseller:</b> ${esc(s.resellerId)}</p>
        <button class="del" data-k="${esc(s.key)}" onclick="deleteStudent(this.dataset.k)">Delete</button>
      </div>
    </div>`).join('') : "<p class='empty'>কোনো স্টুডেন্ট পাওয়া যায়নি। উপরের ফর্ম থেকে যোগ করুন।</p>";
}

function deleteStudent(k){
  if(confirm("Are you sure you want to delete this student?"))
    db.ref('subAdmins/'+currentSubAdminKey+'/students/'+k).remove();
}

function submitWithdrawal(){
  const method = $('wMethod').value, number = $('wNumber').value.trim(), amount = parseFloat($('wAmount').value);
  if(!number){ toast("❌ Payment Number দিন", true); return; }
  if(isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0){ toast("❌ সঠিক পরিমাণ লিখুন", true); return; }
  const bal = parseFloat(currentSubAdminData.balance||0);
  if(amount > bal){ toast("❌ Insufficient Balance", true); return; }
  const date = new Date().toLocaleString(), now = Date.now();
  const wRef = db.ref('subAdminWithdrawals').push(), tRef = db.ref('subAdminTransactions').push();
  const u = {};
  u['subAdmins/'+currentSubAdminKey+'/balance'] = bal - amount;
  u['subAdmins/'+currentSubAdminKey+'/totalWithdrawn'] = parseFloat(currentSubAdminData.totalWithdrawn||0) + amount;
  u['subAdminWithdrawals/'+wRef.key] = {id:wRef.key, subAdminKey:currentSubAdminKey, name:currentSubAdminData.name,
    loginId:currentSubAdminData.loginId, category:currentSubAdminData.category, paymentMethod:method,
    paymentNumber:number, amount, status:'Pending', requestedAt:now, date};
  u['subAdminTransactions/'+tRef.key] = {id:tRef.key, subAdminKey:currentSubAdminKey, type:'Withdrawal Request',
    amount, description:`Requested via ${method} (${number})`, date, timestamp:now};
  db.ref().update(u, err => {
    if(!err){
      toast("✅ Withdrawal Request Submitted & Balance Deducted");
      $('wNumber').value = ''; $('wAmount').value = '';
      boom();
    } else toast("❌ Failed: " + err.message, true);
  });
}

function logout(){
  localStorage.removeItem('subAdminKey');
  currentSubAdminKey = null; currentSubAdminData = null;
  db.ref().off();
  showLoginView();
}
</script>

  বসানোর নিয়ম: আগের অ্যাড-অনগুলোর পরে, </body> এর ঠিক আগে এই পুরো ব্লকটি পেস্ট করুন। ছবি কোডের ভেতরেই আছে, আলাদা হোস্টিং লাগবে না। -->

<script>

function mk(cls){const i=document.createElement('img');i.src=SRC;i.alt='৳';i.className=cls;i.draggable=false;return i;}
/* ১) ওয়ালেট কার্ডে বড় ছবি */
const wc=document.querySelector('.wallet-card');
if(wc&&!wc.querySelector('.tk-img'))wc.appendChild(mk('tk-img'));
/* ২) হোমের My Balance বক্সে ছোট ছবি */
const hb=document.getElementById('homeBalance');
const box=hb&&hb.closest('.dash-box');
if(box&&!box.querySelector('.tk-img')){box.classList.add('tk-host');box.appendChild(mk('tk-img'));}
/* ৩) প্রোফাইলের Current Balance এর পাশে */
const pb=document.getElementById('pBalance');
if(pb&&!pb.parentNode.querySelector('.tk-mini')){pb.parentNode.firstElementChild.prepend(mk('tk-mini'));}
})();

<script>

const q=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let hist={},boundKey=null,ref=null,dirty=false,filled=false,jumped=false;

/* UI inject */

    </div>

  </div>

</div>`);

q('nav').firstElementChild.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<button data-t="target" onclick="switchSubTab(\'target\')"><span>🎯</span>Target</button>');
q('homeSection').children[0].insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<div class="tg-warn bad hide" id="tgHomeWarn" onclick="switchSubTab(\'target\')" style="cursor:pointer">⚠️ আজকের টার্গেট এখনো লেখা হয়নি! প্রতিদিন টার্গেট লেখা বাধ্যতামূলক — এখানে ট্যাপ করুন।</div>');

window.tgAddRow=function(t,v){
  q('tgCustom').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="tg-row"><input class="tc-t" placeholder="টার্গেটের নাম"><input class="tc-v" type="number" min="0" placeholder="সংখ্যা"><button type="button" onclick="this.parentNode.remove()">✕</button></div>');
  const r=q('tgCustom').lastElementChild; if(t!=null){r.querySelector('.tc-t').value=t;r.querySelector('.tc-v').value=v;}
};
['tgLeads','tgCalls','tgSales','tgStudents','tgNote','tgCustom'].forEach(i=>q(i).addEventListener('input',()=>dirty=true));

function esc2(s){return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));}

function render(){
  const d=dk(), t=hist[d];
  q('tgDateTxt').textContent='📅 '+d+' (ঢাকা সময়)';
  const st=q('tgStatus'), hw=q('tgHomeWarn');
  if(t){ st.className='tg-warn ok'; st.innerHTML='✅ আজকের টার্গেট জমা দেওয়া হয়েছে। প্রয়োজনে নিচে পরিবর্তন করে আবার সেভ করতে পারেন।'; hw.classList.add('hide'); }
  else { st.className='tg-warn bad'; st.innerHTML='⚠️ <b>প্রতিদিন টার্গেট লেখা বাধ্যতামূলক।</b><br>আজকের টার্গেট এখনো জমা দেননি — নিচের ফর্মটি পূরণ করুন।'; hw.classList.remove('hide'); }
  const nb=document.querySelector('#nav button[data-t="target"]');
  if(nb){ const dot=nb.querySelector('.tg-dot'); if(!t&&!dot) nb.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<i class="tg-dot">❗</i>'); if(t&&dot) dot.remove(); }
  if(t&&!filled&&!dirty){
    q('tgLeads').value=t.leads;q('tgCalls').value=t.calls;q('tgSales').value=t.sales;q('tgStudents').value=t.students;q('tgNote').value=t.note||'';
    q('tgCustom').innerHTML=''; (t.custom||[]).forEach(c=>tgAddRow(c.title,c.value)); dirty=false; filled=true;
  }
  const days=Object.keys(hist).sort().reverse();
  q('tgHist').innerHTML=days.length?days.map(k=>{const h=hist[k];
    const cu=(h.custom||[]).map(c=>' • '+esc2(c.title)+' '+esc2(c.value)).join('');
    return `<div class="hist"><div class="hi">🎯</div><div><b>${esc2(k)}</b><br><span>লিড ${esc2(h.leads)} • কল ${esc2(h.calls)} • সেল ${esc2(h.sales)} • স্টুডেন্ট ${esc2(h.students)}${cu}${h.note?'<br>📝 '+esc2(h.note):''}</span></div></div>`;}).join(''):"<p class='empty'>এখনো কোনো টার্গেট নেই।</p>";
  if(!t&&!jumped&&boundKey){ jumped=true; switchSubTab('target'); toast('⚠️ আজকের টার্গেট লিখুন — এটি বাধ্যতামূলক',true); }
}

window.saveTarget=function(){
  if(!currentSubAdminKey||!currentSubAdminData) return;
  const f=['tgLeads','tgCalls','tgSales','tgStudents'].map(i=>q(i).value.trim());
  if(f.some(v=>v===''||isNaN(v)||+v<0)){ toast('❌ লিড, কল, সেল ও স্টুডেন্ট — সবগুলো টার্গেট লিখুন (না থাকলে 0)',true); return; }
  const custom=[...q('tgCustom').children].map(r=>({title:r.querySelector('.tc-t').value.trim(),value:r.querySelector('.tc-v').value.trim()})).filter(c=>c.title&&c.value!=='');
  const d=dk(), k=currentSubAdminKey, old=hist[d];
  const rec={subAdminKey:k,name:currentSubAdminData.name,loginId:currentSubAdminData.loginId,category:currentSubAdminData.category,date:d,
    leads:+f[0],calls:+f[1],sales:+f[2],students:+f[3],custom,note:q('tgNote').value.trim(),
    createdAt:old&&old.createdAt?old.createdAt:Date.now(),updatedAt:Date.now()};
  const u={}; u['subAdminTargets/'+k+'/'+d]=rec; u['dailyTargets/'+d+'/'+k]=rec;
  db.ref().update(u,err=>{ if(err) toast('❌ '+err.message,true); else { dirty=false; filled=false; toast('✅ আজকের টার্গেট সেভ হয়েছে'); boom(); } });
};

/* লগইন হলে লিসেনার যুক্ত */
setInterval(()=>{
  if(currentSubAdminKey===boundKey) return;
  if(ref){ try{ref.off();}catch(e){} ref=null; }
  boundKey=currentSubAdminKey; hist={}; dirty=false; filled=false; jumped=false;
  if(!boundKey) return;
  ref=db.ref('subAdminTargets/'+boundKey).orderByKey().limitToLast(7);
  ref.on('value',s=>{ hist={}; s.forEach(c=>{hist[c.key]=c.val();}); render(); });
},700);
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== Sub-Admin: স্টুডেন্ট ভিউ (ক্যাটাগরি অনুযায়ী) =====
  বসানোর নিয়ম: সাব-অ্যাডমিন ফাইলের সবচেয়ে শেষের </script> এর পরে, </body> এর ঠিক আগে এই পুরো ব্লকটি পেস্ট করুন।
  Team Leader / Trainer : অ্যাডমিন যাদের Assign করবে সেই স্টুডেন্টরা My Students-এ দেখাবে
  Senior Team Leader    : সব স্টুডেন্ট (নাম, ID, মোবাইল, Reseller ID, ব্যালেন্স) + Block/Unblock, পাসওয়ার্ড নয়
  Teacher               : সব স্টুডেন্ট, শুধু নাম, Student ID ও মোবাইল -->
<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const S=v=>String(v==null?'':v);
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const ALL=['Senior Team Leader','Teacher'];
const curCat=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const allView=()=>ALL.indexOf(curCat())>-1;
const isSenior=()=>curCat()==='Senior Team Leader';

/* ===== বড় প্যানেলের Firebase (স্টুডেন্ট ডাটা) ===== */
const bigCfg={apiKey:"AIzaSyBNquB4k0OCS0eFWJKuo6GlW4fAcfWhHaI",authDomain:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b.firebaseapp.com",projectId:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b",databaseURL:"https://smart-learning-platform-e3f0b-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com"};
let bigApp=null;
for(const a of firebase.apps){ if(a.name==='bigAdmin') bigApp=a; }
if(!bigApp) bigApp=firebase.initializeApp(bigCfg,'bigAdmin');
const bigDb=bigApp.database();

let mine=[], loaded=false, rmap={}, bigRef=null, rRef=null, shown=60, lastQ='', bound='';

function rid(s){ return S(s.resellerId).trim()!==''?s.resellerId:(rmap[S(s.studentId)]||''); }
function updateCounts(){
  const n=mine.length;
  ['homeTotalStudents','stCount','pStudents'].forEach(id=>{ const e=g(id); if(e) e.innerText=n; });
}

/* ===== ক্যাটাগরি অনুযায়ী স্টুডেন্ট তালিকা ===== */
window.renderStudents=function(){
  const av=allView();
  if(av && globalStudentsCache!==mine){ globalStudentsCache=mine; updateCounts(); }
  const box=g('studentsContainer'); if(!box) return;
  if(av && !loaded){ box.innerHTML="<p class='empty'>স্টুডেন্ট লোড হচ্ছে...</p>"; return; }
  const qv=S(g('studentSearch').value).toLowerCase().trim();
  if(qv!==lastQ){ shown=60; lastQ=qv; }
  const list=globalStudentsCache.filter(s=>
    S(s.name).toLowerCase().includes(qv)||S(s.studentId).toLowerCase().includes(qv)||
    S(s.mobile).includes(qv)||(!qv?false:S(rid(s)).toLowerCase().includes(qv)));
  if(!list.length){ box.innerHTML="<p class='empty'>কোনো স্টুডেন্ট পাওয়া যায়নি।"+(av?'':' অ্যাডমিন Assign করলে বা উপরের ফর্ম থেকে যোগ করলে এখানে দেখাবে।')+"</p>"; return; }
  const sen=isSenior(), teacher=av&&!sen;
  box.innerHTML=list.slice(0,shown).map(s=>{
    let extra='';
    if(teacher){
      extra='';
    } else if(sen){
      const b=!!s.isBlocked;
      extra=`<p><b>Reseller:</b> ${E(rid(s)||'N/A')}</p><p><b>Balance:</b> ৳${E(s.balance||0)}</p>
        <p>${b?'<b style="color:#c0392b">Blocked ❌</b>':'<b style="color:#1e8449">Active ✅</b>'}</p>
        <button class="del" style="background:${b?'#27ae60':'#334155'}" data-k="${E(s.key)}" data-b="${b?1:0}" onclick="srBlock(this.dataset.k,this.dataset.b==='1')">${b?'🔓 Unblock':'🚫 Block'}</button>`;
    } else {
      extra=`<p><b>Reseller:</b> ${E(s.resellerId||'N/A')}</p>`+
        (s.assigned?`<p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">📌 অ্যাডমিন দিয়েছেন</p>`
                   :`<button class="del" data-k="${E(s.key)}" onclick="deleteStudent(this.dataset.k)">Delete</button>`);
    }
    return `<div class="student-card"><div class="av">${E(S(s.name||'?').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase())}</div>
      <div style="min-width:0;word-break:break-word"><h4>${E(s.name)}</h4><p><b>ID:</b> ${E(s.studentId)}</p><p><b>Mobile:</b> ${E(s.mobile)}</p>${extra}</div></div>`;
  }).join('')+(list.length>shown?`<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center"><button class="back-btn" id="srMore">আরও দেখুন (${list.length-shown})</button></div>`:'');
  const m=g('srMore'); if(m) m.onclick=()=>{ shown+=60; window.renderStudents(); };
};

/* ===== Senior Team Leader: Block / Unblock ===== */
window.srBlock=function(k,cur){
  if(!isSenior()) return;
  const u=mine.find(x=>x.key===k);
  if(!confirm((cur?'UNBLOCK':'BLOCK')+' '+(u?u.name:'')+'?')) return;
  bigDb.ref('users/'+k).update({isBlocked:!cur}).catch(e=>toast('❌ '+e.message,true));
};

/* ===== সব স্টুডেন্ট লোড (Senior Team Leader / Teacher) ===== */
function startAll(){
  loaded=false; mine=[];
  const sen=isSenior();
  bigRef=bigDb.ref('users');
  bigRef.on('value',snap=>{
    const arr=[];
    snap.forEach(c=>{
      const u=c.val()||{};
      const o={key:c.key,name:u.name||'',studentId:u.id||'',mobile:u.phone||''};
      if(sen){ o.resellerId=u.resellerId!=null?u.resellerId:''; o.balance=u.balance||0; o.isBlocked=!!u.isBlocked; }
      arr.push(o);
      return false;
    });
    mine=arr.reverse(); loaded=true;
    globalStudentsCache=mine; updateCounts();
    window.renderStudents();
    if(typeof renderExtras==='function') renderExtras();
  },err=>{
    toast('❌ স্টুডেন্ট লোড হয়নি: '+err.message,true);
    g('studentsContainer').innerHTML="<p class='empty' style='color:#c0392b'>❌ স্টুডেন্ট লোড হয়নি: "+E(err.message)+"</p>";
  });
  if(sen){
    rRef=db.ref('resellers');
    rRef.on('value',snap=>{
      rmap={};
      snap.forEach(c=>{ const r=c.val()||{}; if(r.studentId) rmap[S(r.studentId)]=r.resellerId; return false; });
      window.renderStudents();
    },()=>{});
  }
}
function stopAll(){
  if(bigRef){ try{bigRef.off();}catch(e){} bigRef=null; }
  if(rRef){ try{rRef.off();}catch(e){} rRef=null; }
  if(globalStudentsCache===mine) globalStudentsCache=[];
  mine=[]; loaded=false; rmap={};
}

/* ===== ক্যাটাগরি অনুযায়ী UI ===== */
function applyUI(){
  const av=allView();
  const f=g('sName'), card=f&&f.closest('.card');
  if(card) card.classList.toggle('hide',av);
  const h=document.querySelector('#studentsSection .sec-hero h3'); if(h) h.textContent=av?'All Students':'My Students';
  const l=document.querySelector('#homeSection .dash-box[onclick*="students"] p'); if(l) l.textContent=av?'All Students':'My Students';
}

/* লগইন / লগআউট / ক্যাটাগরি বদলালে নিজে থেকে চালু হবে */
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, sig=k?k+'|'+curCat():'';
  if(sig===bound) return;
  stopAll(); bound=sig;
  applyUI();
  if(k && allView()) startAll();
},600);
}catch(e){ console.error('Student add-on error:',e); }
})();
</script>

  Team Leader / Trainer : যে অর্ডারে তাদের টিক দেওয়া হয়েছে শুধু সেগুলো দেখবে, Approve / Reject করতে পারবে
  Senior Team Leader    : সব অর্ডার দেখবে ও কোন ধাপে আছে জানবে (শুধু দেখা) -->

<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const S=v=>String(v==null?'':v);
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const ST={TeamReview:['🕐 টিমের রিভিউতে','#f08c00'],Pending:['🟡 অ্যাডমিন Pending','#f1c40f'],Processing:['🔵 Processing','#3498db'],Shipping:['🟣 Shipping','#9b59b6'],Delivered:['🟢 Delivered','#2ecc71'],Returned:['🔴 Returned','#e74c3c'],Cancelled:['❌ Cancelled','#e74c3c']};
const STEPS=['TeamReview','Pending','Processing','Shipping','Delivered'];
const STEP_LBL=['টিম রিভিউ','অ্যাডমিন','প্রসেসিং','শিপিং','ডেলিভারড'];
const CHIPS=['All','TeamReview','Pending','Processing','Shipping','Delivered','Returned','Cancelled'];
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isSenior=()=>role()==='Senior Team Leader';
const canUse=()=>['Team Leader','Trainer','Senior Team Leader'].indexOf(role())>-1;

/* ---------- ডাটা ---------- */
function getLinks(o){
  let a=[];
  if(Array.isArray(o.imageLinks)) a=o.imageLinks;
  else if(o.imageLinks&&typeof o.imageLinks==='object') a=Object.values(o.imageLinks);
  else if(typeof o.image==='string'&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(o.image)) a=[o.image];
  return a.filter(u=>typeof u==='string'&&/^https?:\/\/\S+$/i.test(u.trim())).map(u=>u.trim());
}
function stop(){
  refs.forEach(r=>{try{r.off();}catch(e){}}); refs=[]; attached={};
  if(idxRef){try{idxRef.off();}catch(e){} idxRef=null;}
  if(bigRef){try{bigRef.off();}catch(e){} bigRef=null;}
  orders={};
}
function startTeam(key){
  idxRef=db.ref('team_order_index/'+key);
  idxRef.on('value',snap=>{
    const ids={};
    snap.forEach(c=>{ ids[c.key]=true; return false; });
    Object.keys(attached).forEach(id=>{ if(!ids[id]){ try{attached[id].off();}catch(e){} delete attached[id]; delete orders[id]; } });
    Object.keys(ids).forEach(id=>{
      if(attached[id]) return;
      const r=db.ref('smart_product_orders/'+id);
      attached[id]=r; refs.push(r);
      r.on('value',s=>{ if(s.exists()) orders[id]=s.val(); else delete orders[id]; render(); });
    });
    render();
  });
}
function startSenior(){
  bigRef=db.ref('smart_product_orders').orderByChild('timestamp').limitToLast(150);
  bigRef.on('value',snap=>{
    orders={};
    snap.forEach(c=>{ orders[c.key]=c.val(); return false; });
    render();
  });
}

/* ---------- রেন্ডার ---------- */
function mine(o){ return isSenior()||!!(o.teamKeys&&o.teamKeys[currentSubAdminKey]); }
function list(){ return Object.keys(orders).map(k=>orders[k]).filter(o=>o&&mine(o)).sort((a,b)=>(b.timestamp||0)-(a.timestamp||0)); }

window.odFilter=function(f){ filter=f; render(); };

function card(o){
  const s=o.status||'Pending', st=ST[s]||[s,'#999'];
  const team=(o.team||[]).map(t=>E(t.name)+' ('+short(t.category)+')').join(', ')||'—';
  const links=getLinks(o).map((u,i)=>'<a href="'+E(u)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style="color:var(--p);font-weight:700">🔗 Link '+(i+1)+'</a>').join(' &nbsp; ');
  const idx=STEPS.indexOf(s);
  let trk='';
  if(idx>=0){
    let pts='';
    STEPS.forEach((x,i)=>{
      pts+='<div style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;flex:none;background:'+(i<=idx?'#2ecc71':'var(--line)')+'"></div>';
      if(i<STEPS.length-1) pts+='<div style="flex:1;height:3px;background:'+(i<idx?'#2ecc71':'var(--line)')+'"></div>';
    });
    trk='<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;margin:10px 4px 2px">'+pts+'</div><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:9.5px;color:var(--mute)">'+STEP_LBL.map(x=>'<span>'+x+'</span>').join('')+'</div>';
  }
  const rb=o.reviewedBy||{};
  let rev='';
  if(s==='Cancelled'&&o.cancelledBy==='team') rev='<div class="od-note bad">❌ '+E(rb.name)+' ('+short(rb.category)+') Reject করেছেন'+(o.rejectReason?'<br>কারণ: '+E(o.rejectReason):'')+'</div>';
  else if(o.teamStatus==='Approved'&&rb.name) rev='<div class="od-note ok">✅ '+E(rb.name)+' ('+short(rb.category)+') Approve করেছেন'+(o.reviewedDate?' • '+E(o.reviewedDate):'')+'</div>';
  const canAct=!isSenior()&&s==='TeamReview'&&o.teamKeys&&o.teamKeys[currentSubAdminKey];
  const act=canAct?'<div class="od-act"><button style="background:#27ae60" data-o="'+E(o.orderId)+'" onclick="odDecide(this.dataset.o,true)">✅ Approve</button><button style="background:#e74c3c" data-o="'+E(o.orderId)+'" onclick="odDecide(this.dataset.o,false)">❌ Reject</button></div>':'';
  return '<div class="od" style="--oc:'+st[1]+'">'
   +'<div class="od-h"><b>📦 '+E(o.productName)+'</b><span class="od-b" style="background:'+st[1]+'">'+st[0]+'</span></div>'
   +'<div><b>Order ID:</b> '+E(o.orderId)+' &nbsp;|&nbsp; <b>Student:</b> '+E(o.studentId)+'<br><b>তারিখ:</b> '+E(o.date||'')+'</div>'
   +'<div class="od-box"><b>Qty:</b> '+E(o.qty)+' | <b>Size:</b> '+E(o.size)+' | <b>Color:</b> '+E(o.color)+(o.notes?'<br><b>নোট:</b> '+E(o.notes):'')
   +'<br><b>কাস্টমার:</b> '+E(o.name)+' ('+E(o.phone)+')<br><b>ঠিকানা:</b> '+E(o.address)+', '+E(o.thana)+', '+E(o.district)+'</div>'
   +'<div class="od-box"><b>মূল্য:</b> পাইকারি ৳'+E(o.wholesaleTotal!=null?o.wholesaleTotal:o.wholesalePrice)+' | খুচরা ৳'+E(o.retailTotal!=null?o.retailTotal:o.retailPrice)+' | বিক্রয় ৳'+E(o.soldTotal!=null?o.soldTotal:o.soldPrice)+' | ডেলিভারি ৳'+E(o.deliveryCharge)+'<br><b>লাভ:</b> ৳'+E(o.profit)+'</div>'
   +(links?'<div style="margin-top:8px">'+links+'</div>':'')
   +'<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:12px"><b>টিম:</b> '+team+'</div>'
   +trk+rev+act+'</div>';
}

function render(){
  if(!canUse()) return;
  const all=list();
  const q=S(g('odSearch').value).toLowerCase().trim();
  const cnt={All:all.length};
  all.forEach(o=>{ const s=o.status||'Pending'; cnt[s]=(cnt[s]||0)+1; });
  g('odChips').innerHTML=CHIPS.map(c=>'<button class="'+(filter===c?'on':'')+'" onclick="odFilter(\''+c+'\')">'+(c==='All'?'সব':ST[c][0])+' ('+(cnt[c]||0)+')</button>').join('');
  const f=all.filter(o=>(filter==='All'||(o.status||'Pending')===filter)&&(!q||[o.orderId,o.studentId,o.productName,o.name,o.phone].some(v=>S(v).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1)));
  g('odList').innerHTML=f.length?f.map(card).join(''):"<p class='empty'>কোনো অর্ডার পাওয়া যায়নি।</p>";
  const w=cnt.TeamReview||0;
  g('odSub').innerText=isSenior()?'সব অর্ডার ও তাদের বর্তমান ধাপ':'আপনার কাছে '+w+'টি অর্ডার রিভিউর অপেক্ষায়';
  g('homeOrdersLbl').innerText=(isSenior()?'All Orders':'Team Orders')+(w?' ('+w+')':'');
  const nb=g('navOrders'), dot=nb.querySelector('.od-dot');
  if(w&&!dot) nb.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<i class="od-dot">'+w+'</i>');
  else if(w&&dot) dot.textContent=w;
  else if(!w&&dot) dot.remove();
}

/* ---------- Approve / Reject ---------- */
window.odDecide=function(oid,ok){
  if(isSenior()||!currentSubAdminData) return;
  let reason='';
  if(ok){ if(!confirm('অর্ডারটি Approve করবেন? এটি অ্যাডমিনের কাছে Pending হিসেবে চলে যাবে।')) return; }
  else { reason=prompt('বাতিলের কারণ লিখুন (ঐচ্ছিক):'); if(reason===null) return; }
  const me={key:currentSubAdminKey,name:S(currentSubAdminData.name),category:S(currentSubAdminData.category)};
  db.ref('smart_product_orders/'+oid).transaction(cur=>{
    if(cur===null) return cur;
    if(cur.status!=='TeamReview') return;
    if(!(cur.teamKeys&&cur.teamKeys[me.key])) return;
    const u=ok?{status:'Pending',teamStatus:'Approved'}
             :{status:'Cancelled',teamStatus:'Rejected',cancelledBy:'team',cancelledAt:Date.now(),rejectReason:S(reason).trim()};
    return Object.assign({},cur,u,{reviewedBy:me,reviewedAt:Date.now(),reviewedDate:new Date().toLocaleString()});
  },(err,committed)=>{
    if(err){ toast('❌ '+err.message,true); return; }
    if(!committed){ toast('⚠️ অর্ডারটি আগেই অন্য কেউ রিভিউ করেছে',true); return; }
    toast(ok?'✅ Approve হয়েছে, অ্যাডমিনের কাছে গেছে':'❌ অর্ডার Reject করা হয়েছে');
  },false);
};

/* লগইন / লগআউট / ক্যাটাগরি বদলালে নিজে থেকে চালু হবে */
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, sig=k?k+'|'+role():'';
  if(sig===bound) return;
  stop(); bound=sig;
  const ok=!!k&&canUse();
  g('navOrders').classList.toggle('hide',!ok);
  g('homeOrdersBox').classList.toggle('hide',!ok);
  if(!ok) return;
  filter=isSenior()?'All':'TeamReview';
  g('odTitle').innerText=isSenior()?'All Product Orders':'Team Orders';
  if(isSenior()) startSenior(); else startTeam(k);
  render();
},600);
}catch(e){ console.error('Orders add-on error:',e); }
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== Sub-Admin অ্যাড-অন: (১) Senior Team Leader-এর Team Targets ভিউ (২) স্টুডেন্টের পাসওয়ার্ড + WhatsApp বাটন =====
  বসানোর নিয়ম: ফাইলের সবচেয়ে শেষের </script> এর পরে, </body> এর ঠিক আগে এই পুরো ব্লকটি পেস্ট করুন। -->
<style>

<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const S=v=>String(v==null?'':v);
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const dk=()=>new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'});
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isSen=()=>role()==='Senior Team Leader';

/* =====================================================
   পর্ব ১: Senior Team Leader — Team Targets
   ===================================================== */
g('dashboardView').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
<div id="teamTargetsSection" class="section">
  <button class="back-btn" onclick="switchSubTab('home')">← ফিরে যান (Back)</button>
  <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">📊</span><div><h3>Team Targets</h3><p>টিম লিডার, ট্রেইনার ও টিচারদের টার্গেট</p></div></div>
  <div class="card">
    <div class="form-group" style="margin:0"><label>📅 তারিখ বাছাই করুন</label><input type="date" id="ttDate"></div>
    <div class="tt-chips" id="ttChips"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="tt-sum" id="ttSum"></div>
  <div id="ttList"></div>
</div>`);

let ttRef=null, ttSig='', ttDate=dk(), ttData={}, ttCat='All';
g('ttDate').value=ttDate;
g('ttDate').addEventListener('change',e=>{ ttDate=e.target.value||dk(); });

const ORDER=['Team Leader','Trainer','Teacher'];
window.ttSetCat=function(c){ ttCat=c; ttRender(); };

function ttCard(r){
  const cu=(r.custom||[]).map(c=>'<span class="tt-x">'+E(c.title)+': <b>'+E(c.value)+'</b></span>').join('');
  const tm=r.updatedAt?new Date(r.updatedAt).toLocaleTimeString('en-US',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'';
  return '<div class="tt-p"><div class="tt-h"><div><b>'+E(r.name)+'</b><br><small style="color:var(--mute)">ID: '+E(r.loginId)+(tm?' • জমা: '+tm:'')+'</small></div><span class="tt-cat">'+E(r.category)+'</span></div>'
   +'<div class="tt-g"><div><b>'+E(r.leads)+'</b>🎯 লিড</div><div><b>'+E(r.calls)+'</b>📞 কল</div><div><b>'+E(r.sales)+'</b>🛒 সেল</div><div><b>'+E(r.students)+'</b>👥 স্টুডেন্ট</div></div>'
   +(cu?'<div>'+cu+'</div>':'')
   +(r.note?'<div class="tt-note">📝 '+E(r.note)+'</div>':'')+'</div>';
}

function ttRender(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const all=Object.keys(ttData).map(k=>Object.assign({key:k},ttData[k]))
    .filter(r=>r&&r.category&&r.category!=='Senior Team Leader')
    .sort((a,b)=>(ORDER.indexOf(a.category)-ORDER.indexOf(b.category))||S(a.name).localeCompare(S(b.name)));
  const cnt={All:all.length}; ORDER.forEach(c=>cnt[c]=all.filter(r=>r.category===c).length);
  g('ttChips').innerHTML=['All'].concat(ORDER).map(c=>'<button class="'+(ttCat===c?'on':'')+'" onclick="ttSetCat(this.dataset.c)" data-c="'+c+'">'+(c==='All'?'সব':c)+' ('+cnt[c]+')</button>').join('');
  const f=ttCat==='All'?all:all.filter(r=>r.category===ttCat);
  const sum=k=>f.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r[k]||0),0);
  g('ttSum').innerHTML='<div><b>'+sum('leads')+'</b>🎯 মোট লিড</div><div><b>'+sum('calls')+'</b>📞 মোট কল</div><div><b>'+sum('sales')+'</b>🛒 মোট সেল</div><div><b>'+sum('students')+'</b>👥 মোট স্টুডেন্ট</div>';
  g('ttList').innerHTML=f.length?f.map(ttCard).join(''):"<p class='empty'>এই তারিখে কেউ টার্গেট জমা দেয়নি।</p>";
}

/* Senior-এর জন্য পুরনো Daily Target বাটন/ওয়ার্নিং বদলে Team Targets */
const _sw=window.switchSubTab;
window.switchSubTab=function(t,n){ if(t==='target'&&isSen()) t='teamTargets'; return _sw(t,n); };
const _toast=window.toast;
window.toast=function(m,e){ if(isSen()&&S(m).indexOf('আজকের টার্গেট লিখুন')>-1) return; return _toast(m,e); };
const hs=document.createElement('style');
hs.textContent='body[data-theme="Senior Team Leader"] #tgHomeWarn,body[data-theme="Senior Team Leader"] .tg-dot{display:none!important}';
document.head.appendChild(hs);

function relabel(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const nb=document.querySelector('#nav button[data-t="target"]');
  if(nb){ nb.setAttribute('data-t','teamTargets'); nb.setAttribute('onclick',"switchSubTab('teamTargets')"); nb.innerHTML='<span>📊</span>Targets'; }
  const hb=document.querySelector('#homeSection .dash-box[onclick*="target"]');
  if(hb){ hb.innerHTML='<div class="ic">📊</div><p>Team Targets</p>'; }
}

/* ===== ট্যাব ও লিসেনার অটো-কানেক্ট ===== */
setInterval(()=>{
  relabel();
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, sig=(k&&isSen())?k+'|'+ttDate:'';
  if(sig===ttSig) return;
  if(ttRef){ try{ttRef.off();}catch(e){} ttRef=null; }
  ttSig=sig; ttData={};
  if(!sig) return;
  ttRef=db.ref('dailyTargets/'+ttDate);
  ttRef.on('value',s=>{ ttData=s.val()||{}; ttRender(); },err=>{
    g('ttList').innerHTML="<p class='empty' style='color:#c0392b'>❌ টার্গেট লোড হয়নি: "+E(err.message)+"</p>";
  });
},600);

/* =====================================================
   পর্ব ২: স্টুডেন্টের পাসওয়ার্ড + WhatsApp বাটন
   (Team Leader / Trainer / Senior Team Leader / Teacher — সবার জন্য)
   ===================================================== */
let pw={}, pwRef=null, pwSig='', pwLoaded=false;

function waNum(n){
  let d=S(n).replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(!d) return '';
  if(d.indexOf('00')===0) d=d.slice(2);
  else if(d.indexOf('01')===0) d='88'+d;          // 01XXXXXXXXX -> 8801XXXXXXXXX
  else if(d.length===10&&d[0]==='1') d='880'+d;
  return d;
}

function enhance(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#studentsContainer .student-card').forEach(c=>{
    if(c.querySelector('.pw-x')) return;
    let id='', mob='', mobP=null;
    c.querySelectorAll('p').forEach(p=>{
      const b=p.querySelector('b'); if(!b) return;
      const l=b.textContent.trim(), v=p.textContent.replace(b.textContent,'').trim();
      if(l==='ID:') id=v; else if(l==='Mobile:'){ mob=v; mobP=p; }
    });
    if(!mobP) return;
    const rec=pw[id]||{};
    const pass=rec.pw;
    const num=waNum(mob||rec.ph);
    let h='<p class="pw-x"><b>🔑 Password:</b> ';
    if(!pwLoaded) h+='<span style="color:var(--mute)">লোড হচ্ছে...</span>';
    else if(pass==null||pass==='') h+='<span style="color:var(--mute)">N/A</span>';
    else h+='<code>'+E(pass)+'</code> <button class="copy pw-copy" data-v="'+E(pass)+'">Copy</button>';
    h+='</p>';
    if(num) h+='<a class="wa-btn" href="https://wa.me/'+num+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">💬 WhatsApp</a>';
    mobP.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',h);
  });
}

/* আগের renderStudents-এর পরে পাসওয়ার্ড ও WhatsApp বাটন যোগ */
const _rs=window.renderStudents;
window.renderStudents=function(){ _rs.apply(this,arguments); try{enhance();}catch(e){console.error(e);} };

document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('.pw-copy'); if(!b) return;
  const v=b.dataset.v||'';
  (navigator.clipboard?navigator.clipboard.writeText(v):Promise.reject()).then(()=>toast('✅ Password কপি হয়েছে'),()=>toast('❌ কপি করা যায়নি',true));
});

/* বড় প্যানেলের ডাটাবেস থেকে পাসওয়ার্ড লোড */
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, sig=k?k+'|'+role():'';
  if(sig===pwSig) return;
  if(pwRef){ try{pwRef.off();}catch(e){} pwRef=null; }
  pwSig=sig; pw={}; pwLoaded=false;
  if(!k) return;
  let app=null; for(const a of firebase.apps){ if(a.name==='bigAdmin') app=a; }
  if(!app){ pwSig=''; return; }   // স্টুডেন্ট অ্যাড-অন তৈরি করলে পরের চক্রে আবার চেষ্টা করবে
  pwRef=app.database().ref('users');
  pwRef.on('value',snap=>{
    const m={};
    snap.forEach(c=>{
      const u=c.val()||{};
      m[S(u.id)]={ pw:(u.password!=null?u.password:(u.pass!=null?u.pass:u.pin)), ph:u.phone };
      return false;
    });
    pw=m; pwLoaded=true;
    if(typeof window.renderStudents==='function') window.renderStudents();
  },err=>{ pwLoaded=true; toast('❌ পাসওয়ার্ড লোড হয়নি: '+err.message,true); });
},700);

}catch(e){ console.error('Targets/Password add-on error:',e); }
})();
</script>

<!-- ===== (২) সাব-অ্যাডমিন প্যানেল: Senior Team Leader-এর "স্টুডেন্ট নোটিশ" =====
  বসানোর নিয়ম: সাব-অ্যাডমিন ফাইলের একদম শেষে, </body> এর ঠিক আগে পেস্ট করুন। -->
<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id),S=v=>String(v==null?'':v);
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isSen=()=>role()==='Senior Team Leader';

g('dashboardView').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
<div id="stuNoticeSection" class="section">
  <button class="back-btn" onclick="switchSubTab('home')">← ফিরে যান (Back)</button>
  <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">📣</span><div><h3>স্টুডেন্ট নোটিশ</h3><p>সব স্টুডেন্টের কাছে নোটিশ পাঠান</p></div></div>
  <div class="card">
    <h3>✍️ নতুন নোটিশ</h3>
    <div class="form-group"><label>শিরোনাম</label><input type="text" id="snTitle" placeholder="নোটিশের শিরোনাম"></div>
    <div class="form-group"><label>বার্তা</label><textarea id="snMsg" rows="4" class="note" placeholder="স্টুডেন্টদের উদ্দেশ্যে বার্তা লিখুন..."></textarea></div>
    <button class="btn-primary" onclick="snSend()">📤 সব স্টুডেন্টকে পাঠান</button>
  </div>
  <div class="card"><h3>📜 পাঠানো নোটিশ</h3><div id="snList" style="margin-top:10px"></div></div>
</div>`);
g('homeSection').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="dash-box hide" id="snHomeBox" onclick="switchSubTab(\'stuNotice\')"><div class="ic">📣</div><p>স্টুডেন্ট নোটিশ</p></div>');

window.snSend=function(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const t=g('snTitle').value.trim(), m=g('snMsg').value.trim();
  if(!t||!m){ toast('❌ শিরোনাম ও বার্তা দিন',true); return; }
  if(!confirm('এই নোটিশ সব স্টুডেন্টের কাছে যাবে। পাঠাবেন?')) return;
  const me=currentSubAdminData||{};
  db.ref('student_notices').push({title:t,message:m,from:S(me.name),fromRole:'Senior Team Leader',fromKey:currentSubAdminKey,ts:Date.now()},err=>{
    if(err){ toast('❌ '+err.message,true); return; }
    toast('✅ নোটিশ পাঠানো হয়েছে'); g('snTitle').value=''; g('snMsg').value='';
    try{ db.ref('subAdminLogs').push({type:'broadcast',text:'স্টুডেন্ট নোটিশ: '+t,actorKey:currentSubAdminKey,actorName:S(me.name),actorCategory:'Senior Team Leader',ts:Date.now()}); }catch(e){}
    if(typeof boom==='function') boom();
  });
};
window.snDel=function(id){ if(isSen()&&confirm('নোটিশটি মুছবেন? স্টুডেন্টরাও আর দেখবে না।')) db.ref('student_notices/'+id).remove(); };

let ref=null,bound='';
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, sig=(k&&currentSubAdminData)?k+'|'+role():'';
  if(sig===bound) return;
  if(ref){ try{ref.off();}catch(e){} ref=null; }
  bound=sig; g('snHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!isSen()); g('snList').innerHTML='';
  if(!sig||!isSen()) return;
  ref=db.ref('student_notices').orderByKey().limitToLast(30);
  ref.on('value',s=>{
    const a=[]; s.forEach(c=>{ a.push(Object.assign({id:c.key},c.val())); });
    a.reverse();
    g('snList').innerHTML=a.map(n=>'<div class="hist" style="display:block"><b>📣 '+E(n.title)+'</b><br>'+E(n.message)+'<br><span>'+E(n.from)+' • '+new Date(n.ts||0).toLocaleString('en-GB',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'})+'</span><br><button class="del" data-i="'+E(n.id)+'" onclick="snDel(this.dataset.i)">Delete</button></div>').join('')||"<p class='empty'>এখনো কোনো নোটিশ পাঠানো হয়নি।</p>";
  });
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('Student notice add-on error:',e); }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id),S=v=>String(v==null?'':v),N=v=>Number(v)||0;
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const dOf=t=>new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'}), dk=()=>dOf(Date.now());
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isSen=()=>role()==='Senior Team Leader';
const me=()=>({key:currentSubAdminKey,name:S(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.name),category:role()});
const sk=id=>S(id).replace(/[.#$\[\]\/]/g,'_');
const STUCK_H=12, INACT_D=7, MEM=['Team Leader','Trainer','Teacher'];
const bigDb=()=>{ for(const a of firebase.apps) if(a.name==='bigAdmin') return a.database(); return null; };
function log(type,text,x){ try{ const m=me(); db.ref('subAdminLogs').push(Object.assign({type,text,actorKey:m.key,actorName:m.name,actorCategory:m.category,ts:Date.now()},x||{})); }catch(e){} }
function ago(t){ if(!t) return '⚪ কখনো না'; const m=Math.floor((Date.now()-t)/6e4); return m<6?'🟢 অনলাইন':m<60?'🟡 '+m+' মি. আগে':m<1440?'🟡 '+Math.floor(m/60)+' ঘ. আগে':'🔴 '+Math.floor(m/1440)+' দিন আগে'; }

/* ---------- ডাটা ---------- */
let staff={},users={},orders={},targets={},assigns={},acts={},tasks={},logs=[],bcast=[],warns=[],stuCnt={};
let refs=[],bound='',tab='dash',range=7,sf='all',sq='',lf='all',selSid='',lastUnread=-1;

/* ---------- UI ইনজেক্ট ---------- */
g('dashboardView').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
<div id="inboxSection" class="section"><button class="back-btn" onclick="switchSubTab('home')">← ফিরে যান (Back)</button>
 <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">📬</span><div><h3>Inbox</h3><p>নোটিশ, সতর্কবার্তা ও টাস্ক</p></div></div><div id="inboxBody"></div></div>
<div id="ccSection" class="section"><button class="back-btn" onclick="switchSubTab('home')">← ফিরে যান (Back)</button>
 <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">👑</span><div><h3>Control Center</h3><p>টিমের নজরদারি ও নিয়ন্ত্রণ</p></div></div>
 <div class="cc-chips" id="ccTabs"></div><div id="ccBody"></div></div>`);
g('homeSection').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="dash-box" onclick="switchSubTab(\'inbox\')"><div class="ic">📬</div><p id="inboxLbl">Inbox</p></div><div class="dash-box hide" id="ccHomeBox" onclick="switchSubTab(\'cc\')"><div class="ic">👑</div><p>Control Center</p></div>');
g('homeSection').children[0].insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<div class="cc-alert hide" id="ccAlert" onclick="switchSubTab(\'cc\')"></div>');
const TABS=[['dash','📊 ড্যাশবোর্ড'],['team','👥 টিম'],['comm','📢 যোগাযোগ'],['stu','🎓 স্টুডেন্ট'],['ord','📦 অর্ডার'],['log','🛡️ লগ']];
const _sw=window.switchSubTab;
window.switchSubTab=function(t,n){ const r=_sw(t,n);
 if(t==='inbox'){ localStorage.setItem('inboxSeen_'+currentSubAdminKey,Date.now()); inboxRender(); }
 if(t==='cc'&&isSen()) ccRender(true); return r; };
window.ccTab=function(t){ tab=t; ccRender(true); };
window.ccSet=function(k,v){ if(k==='range')range=v; if(k==='sf')sf=v; if(k==='lf')lf=v; ccRender(true); };

/* ---------- হিসাব ---------- */
function members(){ return Object.values(staff).filter(s=>MEM.indexOf(s.category)>-1&&s.status!=='Suspended'); }
function stats(n){
 const days=[]; for(let i=0;i<n;i++) days.push(dOf(Date.now()-i*864e5)); const ds=new Set(days);
 return members().map(m=>{ const r={m,days:0,leads:0,calls:0,sales:0,students:0,orders:0,delivered:0,profit:0,pend:0,stu:stuCnt[m.key]||0};
  days.forEach(d=>{ const t=(targets[d]||{})[m.key]; if(t){ r.days++; r.leads+=N(t.leads); r.calls+=N(t.calls); r.sales+=N(t.sales); r.students+=N(t.students); } });
  Object.values(orders).forEach(o=>{ if(!o||!o.teamKeys||!o.teamKeys[m.key]||!ds.has(dOf(o.timestamp))||o.status==='Cancelled') return;
   r.orders++; if(o.status==='Delivered'){ r.delivered++; r.profit+=N(o.profit); } if(o.status==='TeamReview') r.pend++; });
  r.pts=r.days*2+r.orders*5+r.delivered*5; return r; });
}
function stuck(){ return Object.values(orders).filter(o=>o&&o.status==='TeamReview'&&Date.now()-N(o.timestamp)>STUCK_H*36e5); }
const tiles=a=>'<div class="cc-t">'+a.map(x=>'<div><b>'+x[0]+'</b>'+x[1]+'</div>').join('')+'</div>';
const chips=(k,cur,arr)=>'<div class="cc-chips">'+arr.map(a=>'<button class="'+(cur==a[0]?'on':'')+'" onclick="ccSet(\''+k+'\','+(typeof a[0]==='number'?a[0]:'\''+a[0]+'\'')+')">'+a[1]+'</button>').join('')+'</div>';

/* ---------- ১) ড্যাশবোর্ড ---------- */
const T={};
T.dash=()=>{
 const tod=targets[dk()]||{}, now=stats(1), rep=stats(range).sort((a,b)=>b.pts-a.pts);
 const sub=now.filter(x=>tod[x.m.key]), non=now.filter(x=>!tod[x.m.key]);
 let h=tiles([[sub.length,'✅ আজ জমা'],[non.length,'❌ জমা দেয়নি'],[stuck().length,'⏰ আটকে অর্ডার']]);
 h+='<div class="cc-h">✅ আজকের টার্গেট বনাম ফলাফল</div>'+(sub.length?sub.map(x=>{ const t=tod[x.m.key]; return '<div class="cc-c ok"><b>'+E(x.m.name)+'</b> <span class="cc-s">'+E(x.m.category)+'</span><br>🎯 লিড '+E(t.leads)+' • 📞 কল '+E(t.calls)+'<br>🛒 সেল টার্গেট '+E(t.sales)+' → <b>অর্ডার '+x.orders+'</b><br>👥 স্টুডেন্ট টার্গেট '+E(t.students)+' → <b>মোট অ্যাসাইনড '+x.stu+'</b></div>'; }).join(''):'<p class="empty">কেউ জমা দেয়নি।</p>');
 h+='<div class="cc-h">❌ আজ জমা দেয়নি</div><div class="cc-c bad">'+(non.map(x=>E(x.m.name)+' <span class="cc-s">('+E(x.m.category)+')</span>').join('<br>')||'সবাই জমা দিয়েছে 🎉')+'</div>';
 h+='<div class="cc-s">লিড/কল সিস্টেমে ট্র্যাক হয় না, তাই সেগুলো শুধু টার্গেট হিসেবে দেখানো হয়।</div>';
 h+='<div class="cc-h">🏆 রিপোর্ট ও র‍্যাংকিং</div>'+chips('range',range,[[7,'সাপ্তাহিক'],[30,'মাসিক']]);
 h+=rep.map((x,i)=>'<div class="cc-c"><b>'+(['🥇','🥈','🥉'][i]||'#'+(i+1))+' '+E(x.m.name)+'</b> <span class="cc-s">'+E(x.m.category)+'</span> <span class="cc-b" style="float:right;background:var(--p)">'+x.pts+' pt</span><br><span class="cc-s">টার্গেট জমা '+x.days+' দিন • লিড '+x.leads+' • কল '+x.calls+' • সেল '+x.sales+' • স্টুডেন্ট '+x.students+'<br>অর্ডার '+x.orders+' • ডেলিভারড '+x.delivered+'</span></div>').join('')||'<p class="empty">ডাটা নেই।</p>';
 return h+'<div class="cc-s">পয়েন্ট = টার্গেট জমার দিন×২ + অর্ডার×৫ + ডেলিভারড×৫</div>';
};

/* ---------- ২) টিম ম্যানেজমেন্ট ---------- */
T.team=()=>{
 const tod=targets[dk()]||{};
 let h='<div class="cc-h">👥 সদস্য তালিকা</div>'+members().map(m=>{ const a=acts[m.key]; return '<div class="cc-c"><b>'+E(m.name)+'</b> <span class="cc-s">'+E(m.category)+' • '+E(m.loginId)+'</span><br>'+ago(a&&a.lastSeen)+' • স্টুডেন্ট '+(stuCnt[m.key]||0)+' • আজকের টার্গেট '+(tod[m.key]?'✅':'❌')+'<br><button class="cc-btn" style="background:#e67e22" data-k="'+E(m.key)+'" onclick="ccWarn(this.dataset.k)">⚠️ সতর্কবার্তা</button><button class="cc-btn" data-k="'+E(m.key)+'" onclick="ccTask(this.dataset.k)">📌 টাস্ক</button></div>'; }).join('');
 h+='<div class="cc-h">🔁 স্টুডেন্ট অ্যাসাইন / পরিবর্তন</div><div class="cc-c"><input class="cc-in" id="ccFind" placeholder="🔍 স্টুডেন্টের নাম / ID / মোবাইল" oninput="ccFind(this.value)"><div id="ccFindRes"></div></div>';
 return h;
};
window.ccFind=function(v){ v=S(v).toLowerCase().trim(); const box=g('ccFindRes'); if(!v){ box.innerHTML=''; return; }
 const a=Object.values(users).filter(u=>(S(u.name)+S(u.id)+S(u.phone)).toLowerCase().indexOf(v)>-1).slice(0,6);
 box.innerHTML=a.map(u=>{ const cur=assigns[sk(u.id)]||{}; const ks=Object.keys(cur);
  const opts=members().filter(m=>m.category!=='Teacher').map(m=>'<option value="'+E(m.key)+'">'+E(m.name)+' ('+E(m.category)+')</option>').join('');
  return '<div class="cc-c"><b>'+E(u.name)+'</b> <span class="cc-s">'+E(u.id)+' • '+E(u.phone)+'</span><br>বর্তমান: '+(ks.map(k=>E((cur[k]||{}).name)+' ('+E((cur[k]||{}).category)+') <button class="cc-btn" style="background:#e74c3c" data-s="'+E(u.id)+'" data-k="'+E(k)+'" onclick="ccUnassign(this.dataset.s,this.dataset.k)">✕</button>').join(', ')||'কেউ নেই')+'<br><select class="cc-in" id="as_'+E(sk(u.id))+'">'+opts+'</select><button class="cc-btn" data-s="'+E(u.id)+'" onclick="ccAssign(this.dataset.s)">✅ অ্যাসাইন করুন</button></div>'; }).join('')||'<p class="empty">পাওয়া যায়নি।</p>';
};
window.ccAssign=function(sid){ const sel=g('as_'+sk(sid)); const st=sel&&staff[sel.value], u=users[sid]; if(!st||!u) return;
 const b=assigns[sk(sid)]||{}, up={};
 Object.keys(b).forEach(k=>{ if((b[k]||{}).category===st.category){ up['studentAssignments/'+sk(sid)+'/'+k]=null; up['subAdmins/'+k+'/students/'+sk(sid)]=null; } });
 up['studentAssignments/'+sk(sid)+'/'+st.key]={key:st.key,name:st.name,category:st.category,loginId:st.loginId,assignedAt:Date.now(),assignedBy:me().name};
 up['subAdmins/'+st.key+'/students/'+sk(sid)]={name:u.name,studentId:u.id,mobile:u.phone,resellerId:u.resellerId||'',assigned:true,createdAt:Date.now()};
 db.ref().update(up,e=>{ if(e) toast('❌ '+e.message,true); else { toast('✅ অ্যাসাইন হয়েছে'); log('assign',u.name+' ('+u.id+') → '+st.name+' ('+st.category+')'); ccFind(g('ccFind').value); } }); };
window.ccUnassign=function(sid,k){ if(!confirm('অ্যাসাইন সরাবেন?')) return; const up={}; up['studentAssignments/'+sk(sid)+'/'+k]=null; up['subAdmins/'+k+'/students/'+sk(sid)]=null;
 db.ref().update(up,e=>{ if(e) toast('❌ '+e.message,true); else { toast('✅ সরানো হয়েছে'); log('unassign',sid+' ← '+((staff[k]||{}).name||k)); ccFind(g('ccFind').value); } }); };
window.ccWarn=function(k){ const m=prompt('সতর্কবার্তা লিখুন ('+((staff[k]||{}).name||'')+'):'); if(!m||!m.trim()) return;
 db.ref('subAdminNotices/'+k).push({type:'warning',message:m.trim(),from:me().name,ts:Date.now()}).then(()=>{ toast('✅ সতর্কবার্তা পাঠানো হয়েছে'); log('warning',((staff[k]||{}).name||k)+': '+m.trim()); }); };
window.ccTask=function(k){ const t=prompt('টাস্কের বিবরণ ('+((staff[k]||{}).name||'')+'):'); if(!t||!t.trim()) return;
 db.ref('subAdminTasks/'+k).push({title:t.trim(),status:'Pending',from:me().name,ts:Date.now()}).then(()=>{ toast('✅ টাস্ক দেওয়া হয়েছে'); log('task',((staff[k]||{}).name||k)+': '+t.trim()); }); };

/* ---------- ৩) যোগাযোগ ---------- */
T.comm=()=>{
 let h='<div class="cc-h">📢 নোটিশ / নির্দেশনা পাঠান</div><div class="cc-c"><select class="cc-in" id="bcAud"><option value="All">সবাই</option>'+MEM.map(c=>'<option>'+c+'</option>').join('')+'</select><input class="cc-in" id="bcTitle" placeholder="শিরোনাম"><textarea class="cc-in" id="bcMsg" rows="3" placeholder="বার্তা"></textarea><button class="cc-btn" onclick="ccSend()">📤 পাঠান</button></div>';
 h+='<div class="cc-h">📌 দেওয়া টাস্ক</div>';
 const all=[]; Object.keys(tasks).forEach(k=>Object.keys(tasks[k]||{}).forEach(id=>all.push(Object.assign({k},tasks[k][id]))));
 all.sort((a,b)=>N(b.ts)-N(a.ts));
 h+=all.slice(0,30).map(t=>'<div class="cc-c '+(t.status==='Done'?'ok':'warn')+'"><b>'+E((staff[t.k]||{}).name||t.k)+'</b>: '+E(t.title)+'<br><span class="cc-s">'+(t.status==='Done'?'✅ সম্পন্ন':'⏳ বাকি')+' • '+E(dOf(t.ts))+'</span></div>').join('')||'<p class="empty">কোনো টাস্ক নেই।</p>';
 return h;
};
window.ccSend=function(){ const a=g('bcAud').value,t=g('bcTitle').value.trim(),m=g('bcMsg').value.trim(); if(!t||!m){ toast('❌ শিরোনাম ও বার্তা দিন',true); return; }
 db.ref('subAdminBroadcasts').push({audience:a,title:t,message:m,from:me().name,ts:Date.now()}).then(()=>{ toast('✅ নোটিশ পাঠানো হয়েছে'); log('broadcast',a+': '+t); g('bcTitle').value=''; g('bcMsg').value=''; }); };

/* ---------- ৪) স্টুডেন্ট নজরদারি ---------- */
const uAct=u=>N(u.lastSeen)||N(u.createdAt);
function stuFilter(u){ const now=Date.now(),d=864e5;
 return sf==='all'||(sf==='new'&&now-N(u.createdAt)<7*d)||(sf==='blk'&&u.isBlocked)||(sf==='ina'&&now-uAct(u)>INACT_D*d)||(sf==='unv'&&u.isVerified!==true); }
T.stu=()=>{ const a=Object.values(users),now=Date.now(),d=864e5;
 let h=tiles([[a.length,'মোট'],[a.filter(u=>now-N(u.createdAt)<7*d).length,'নতুন (৭ দিন)'],[a.filter(u=>u.isBlocked).length,'Blocked'],[a.filter(u=>u.isVerified!==true).length,'Unverified'],[a.filter(u=>u.resellerId).length,'Reseller আছে'],['৳'+a.reduce((s,u)=>s+N(u.balance),0),'মোট ব্যালেন্স']]);
 h+=chips('sf',sf,[['all','সব'],['new','🆕 নতুন'],['blk','🚫 Blocked'],['ina','💤 Inactive '+INACT_D+'+'],['unv','⚠️ Unverified']]);
 return h+'<input class="cc-in" placeholder="🔍 নাম / ID / মোবাইল / Reseller" value="'+E(sq)+'" oninput="ccSq(this.value)"><div id="ccStuList"></div><div class="cc-s">Inactive: স্টুডেন্ট প্যানেলে lastSeen স্ক্রিপ্ট না থাকলে তৈরির তারিখ ধরা হয়।</div>'; };
function stuList(){ const b=g('ccStuList'); if(!b) return; const q=sq.toLowerCase().trim();
 const a=Object.values(users).filter(u=>stuFilter(u)&&(!q||(S(u.name)+S(u.id)+S(u.phone)+S(u.resellerId)).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1)).sort((x,y)=>N(y.createdAt)-N(x.createdAt));
 b.innerHTML=a.slice(0,50).map(u=>{ const as=Object.values(assigns[sk(u.id)]||{}).map(v=>E(v.name)).join(', ')||'—';
  return '<div class="cc-c '+(u.isBlocked?'bad':'')+'"><b>'+E(u.name)+'</b> <span class="cc-s">'+E(u.id)+' • '+E(u.phone)+'</span><br>Reseller: '+E(u.resellerId||'N/A')+' • ব্যালেন্স ৳'+E(u.balance||0)+'<br><span class="cc-s">টিম: '+as+' • '+(u.isBlocked?'🚫 Blocked':'✅ Active')+' • '+(u.isVerified===true?'Verified':'Unverified')+' • শেষ: '+ago(uAct(u))+'</span></div>'; }).join('')||'<p class="empty">কেউ নেই।</p>'; }
window.ccSq=function(v){ sq=v; stuList(); };

/* ---------- ৫) অর্ডার ও আয় ---------- */
T.ord=()=>{ const st=stuck(), r=stats(range).filter(x=>x.m.category!=='Teacher').sort((a,b)=>b.profit-a.profit);
 let h=chips('range',range,[[1,'আজ'],[7,'৭ দিন'],[30,'৩০ দিন']]);
 h+='<div class="cc-h">⏰ রিভিউতে আটকে ('+STUCK_H+' ঘণ্টার বেশি)</div>'+(st.map(o=>'<div class="cc-c bad"><b>'+E(o.productName)+'</b> • '+E(o.orderId)+'<br><span class="cc-s">'+Math.floor((Date.now()-N(o.timestamp))/36e5)+' ঘণ্টা ধরে অপেক্ষায় • '+(o.team||[]).map(t=>E(t.name)).join(', ')+'</span></div>').join('')||'<div class="cc-c ok">কোনো আটকে থাকা অর্ডার নেই ✅</div>');
 h+='<div class="cc-h">💰 টিম অনুযায়ী অর্ডার ও লাভ</div>'+r.map(x=>'<div class="cc-c"><b>'+E(x.m.name)+'</b> <span class="cc-s">'+E(x.m.category)+'</span><br>অর্ডার '+x.orders+' • ডেলিভারড '+x.delivered+' • রিভিউ বাকি '+x.pend+'<br><b>ডেলিভারড লাভ ৳'+x.profit+'</b></div>').join('');
 return h+'<div class="cc-s">একটি অর্ডারে একাধিক সদস্য থাকলে লাভ প্রত্যেকের নামেই দেখানো হয় (যোগ করবেন না)। সর্বশেষ ৩০০ অর্ডার থেকে হিসাব।</div>'; };

/* ---------- ৬) নিরাপত্তা লগ ---------- */
T.log=()=>{ const ev=logs.slice();
 Object.values(orders).forEach(o=>{ if(o&&o.reviewedAt&&o.reviewedBy) ev.push({type:'order',text:o.reviewedBy.name+' অর্ডার '+(o.status==='Cancelled'&&o.cancelledBy==='team'?'Reject':'Approve')+' করেছেন — '+S(o.productName),actorName:o.reviewedBy.name,actorCategory:o.reviewedBy.category,ts:o.reviewedAt}); });
 ev.sort((a,b)=>N(b.ts)-N(a.ts));
 const f=ev.filter(e=>lf==='all'||(lf==='pw'&&e.type==='pw_view')||(lf==='chg'&&e.type!=='pw_view')).slice(0,100);
 const ic={pw_view:'🔑',assign:'🔁',unassign:'🔁',warning:'⚠️',task:'📌',task_done:'✅',broadcast:'📢',block:'🚫',order:'📦'};
 return chips('lf',lf,[['all','সব'],['pw','🔑 পাসওয়ার্ড দেখা'],['chg','✏️ পরিবর্তন']])+(f.map(e=>'<div class="cc-c '+(e.type==='pw_view'?'warn':'')+'"><b>'+(ic[e.type]||'•')+' '+E(e.actorName)+'</b> <span class="cc-s">'+E(e.actorCategory)+'</span><br>'+E(e.text)+'<br><span class="cc-s">'+new Date(N(e.ts)).toLocaleString('en-GB',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'})+'</span></div>').join('')||'<p class="empty">কোনো লগ নেই।</p>'); };

function ccRender(force){ if(!isSen()||!g('ccSection').classList.contains('active')) return;
 const ae=document.activeElement; if(!force&&ae&&/INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT/.test(ae.tagName)&&g('ccBody').contains(ae)) return;
 g('ccTabs').innerHTML=TABS.map(t=>'<button class="'+(tab===t[0]?'on':'')+'" onclick="ccTab(\''+t[0]+'\')">'+t[1]+'</button>').join('');
 g('ccBody').innerHTML=T[tab](); if(tab==='stu') stuList(); }
function homeAlert(){ const a=g('ccAlert'); if(!isSen()){ a.classList.add('hide'); return; }
 const non=stats(1).filter(x=>!(targets[dk()]||{})[x.m.key]).length, st=stuck().length;
 if(non||st){ a.classList.remove('hide'); a.innerHTML='⚠️ '+(non?non+' জন আজ টার্গেট দেয়নি. ':'')+(st?st+'টি অর্ডার '+STUCK_H+'+ ঘণ্টা রিভিউতে আটকে। ':'')+'<br><small>Control Center খুলতে ট্যাপ করুন</small>'; } else a.classList.add('hide'); }

/* ---------- Inbox (সবার জন্য) ---------- */
function myTasks(){ const t=tasks[currentSubAdminKey]||{}; return Object.keys(t).map(id=>Object.assign({id},t[id])).sort((a,b)=>N(b.ts)-N(a.ts)); }
function myBc(){ const c=role(); return bcast.filter(b=>b.audience==='All'||b.audience===c||isSen()); }
function unread(){ const seen=N(localStorage.getItem('inboxSeen_'+currentSubAdminKey)); return warns.filter(w=>N(w.ts)>seen).length+myBc().filter(b=>N(b.ts)>seen).length+myTasks().filter(t=>t.status!=='Done').length; }
function inboxRender(){ const b=g('inboxBody'); if(!b) return;
 const w=warns.map(x=>'<div class="cc-c bad"><b>⚠️ সতর্কবার্তা</b> <span class="cc-s">'+E(x.from)+' • '+E(dOf(x.ts))+'</span><br>'+E(x.message)+'</div>').join('');
 const t=myTasks().map(x=>'<div class="cc-c '+(x.status==='Done'?'ok':'warn')+'"><b>📌 '+E(x.title)+'</b><br><span class="cc-s">'+E(x.from)+' • '+E(dOf(x.ts))+'</span>'+(x.status==='Done'?' ✅':'<br><button class="cc-btn" data-i="'+E(x.id)+'" onclick="ccDone(this.dataset.i)">✅ সম্পন্ন</button>')+'</div>').join('');
 const n=myBc().map(x=>'<div class="cc-c"><b>📢 '+E(x.title)+'</b><br>'+E(x.message)+'<br><span class="cc-s">'+E(x.from)+' • '+E(dOf(x.ts))+'</span></div>').join('');
 b.innerHTML=(w||t||n)?w+t+n:'<p class="empty">কোনো বার্তা নেই।</p>'; }
window.ccDone=function(id){ const t=(tasks[currentSubAdminKey]||{})[id]; db.ref('subAdminTasks/'+currentSubAdminKey+'/'+id).update({status:'Done',doneAt:Date.now()}).then(()=>log('task_done',S(t&&t.title))); };
function badge(){ const n=unread(); g('inboxLbl').textContent='Inbox'+(n?' ('+n+')':''); if(lastUnread>=0&&n>lastUnread) toast('📬 নতুন বার্তা এসেছে'); lastUnread=n; }

/* ---------- স্টুডেন্ট তালিকায় পাসওয়ার্ড (লগসহ) + WhatsApp ---------- */
function waNum(n){ let d=S(n).replace(/\D/g,''); if(!d) return ''; if(d.indexOf('00')===0) d=d.slice(2); else if(d.indexOf('01')===0) d='88'+d; return d; }
function pwMask(){ document.querySelectorAll('#studentsContainer .student-card').forEach(c=>{ if(c.querySelector('.pw-x2')) return;
 let id='',mob='',mp=null; c.querySelectorAll('p').forEach(p=>{ const b=p.querySelector('b'); if(!b) return; const l=b.textContent.trim(),v=p.textContent.replace(b.textContent,'').trim(); if(l==='ID:') id=v; else if(l==='Mobile:'){ mob=v; mp=p; } });
 if(!mp) return; c.querySelectorAll('.pw-x').forEach(x=>x.remove());
 const u=users[id]||{}, num=waNum(mob||u.phone);
 let h='<p class="pw-x2"><b>🔑 Password:</b> '+(u.pass==null?'<span class="cc-s">N/A</span>':'<code data-p="'+E(u.pass)+'" data-s="'+E(id)+'" data-n="'+E(u.name)+'">••••••••</code> <button class="copy pw-show">👁 দেখুন</button>')+'</p>';
 if(num&&!c.querySelector('.wa-btn')) h+='<a class="wa-btn" href="https://wa.me/'+num+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">💬 WhatsApp</a>';
 mp.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',h); }); }
if(typeof window.renderStudents==='function'){ const _rs=window.renderStudents; window.renderStudents=function(){ _rs.apply(this,arguments); try{ pwMask(); }catch(e){} }; }
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{ const b=e.target.closest('.pw-show'); if(!b) return; const c=b.parentNode.querySelector('code'); if(!c) return;
 if(c.textContent.indexOf('•')===0){ c.textContent=c.dataset.p; b.textContent='🙈 লুকান'; log('pw_view',c.dataset.n+' ('+c.dataset.s+') এর পাসওয়ার্ড দেখেছেন',{targetId:c.dataset.s}); }
 else { c.textContent='••••••••'; b.textContent='👁 দেখুন'; } });
if(typeof window.srBlock==='function'&&true){ window.srBlock=function(k,cur){ if(!isSen()) return; const u=users[Object.keys(users).filter(i=>users[i].key===k)[0]]||{};
 if(!confirm((cur?'UNBLOCK':'BLOCK')+' '+S(u.name)+'?')) return; const bd=bigDb(); if(!bd) return;
 bd.ref('users/'+k).update({isBlocked:!cur}).then(()=>log('block',(cur?'Unblock: ':'Block: ')+S(u.name)+' ('+S(u.id)+')')).catch(e=>toast('❌ '+e.message,true)); }; }

/* ---------- লিসেনার (লগইন/লগআউটে অটো) ---------- */
function stop(){ refs.forEach(r=>{ try{ r.off(); }catch(e){} }); refs=[]; staff={};users={};orders={};targets={};assigns={};acts={};tasks={};logs=[];bcast=[];warns=[];stuCnt={}; lastUnread=-1; }
function on(r,f){ refs.push(r); r.on('value',s=>{ try{ f(s); }catch(e){ console.error(e); } }); }
function ping(){ if(currentSubAdminKey&&currentSubAdminData) db.ref('subAdminActivity/'+currentSubAdminKey).set({name:me().name,category:me().category,lastSeen:Date.now()}); }
function refresh(){ ccRender(); inboxRender(); badge(); homeAlert(); }
function start(){ const k=currentSubAdminKey, sen=isSen();
 on(db.ref('subAdminNotices/'+k).limitToLast(30),s=>{ warns=[]; s.forEach(c=>{ warns.push(c.val()); }); warns.reverse(); refresh(); });
 on(db.ref('subAdminBroadcasts').limitToLast(20),s=>{ bcast=[]; s.forEach(c=>{ bcast.push(c.val()); }); bcast.reverse(); refresh(); });
 on(db.ref('subAdminTasks'+(sen?'':'/'+k)),s=>{ if(sen) tasks=s.val()||{}; else tasks[k]=s.val()||{}; refresh(); });
 const bd=bigDb();
 if(bd) on(bd.ref('users'),s=>{ const m={}; s.forEach(c=>{ const u=c.val()||{}; if(u.id) m[u.id]={key:c.key,id:u.id,name:u.name,phone:u.phone,pass:u.password!=null?u.password:u.pass,resellerId:u.resellerId,balance:u.balance,isBlocked:!!u.isBlocked,isVerified:u.isVerified,createdAt:u.createdAt,lastSeen:u.lastSeen}; return false; }); users=m; if(typeof window.renderStudents==='function') window.renderStudents(); refresh(); });
 if(!sen) return;
 on(db.ref('subAdmins'),s=>{ const m={}; s.forEach(c=>{ const v=c.val()||{}; m[c.key]={key:c.key,name:v.name,loginId:v.loginId,category:v.category,status:v.status}; return false; }); staff=m; refresh(); });
 on(db.ref('subAdminActivity'),s=>{ acts=s.val()||{}; refresh(); });
 on(db.ref('dailyTargets').orderByKey().limitToLast(31),s=>{ targets=s.val()||{}; refresh(); });
 on(db.ref('smart_product_orders').orderByChild('timestamp').limitToLast(300),s=>{ orders={}; s.forEach(c=>{ orders[c.key]=c.val(); return false; }); refresh(); });
 on(db.ref('studentAssignments'),s=>{ assigns={}; stuCnt={}; s.forEach(c=>{ const v=c.val()||{}; assigns[c.key]=v; Object.keys(v).forEach(ck=>{ const x=v[ck]||{},kk=x.key||x.subAdminKey||ck; stuCnt[kk]=(stuCnt[kk]||0)+1; }); return false; }); refresh(); });
 on(db.ref('subAdminLogs').orderByKey().limitToLast(150),s=>{ logs=[]; s.forEach(c=>{ logs.push(c.val()); }); refresh(); });
}
setInterval(()=>{ const k=currentSubAdminKey, sig=(k&&currentSubAdminData)?k+'|'+role():''; if(sig===bound) return;
 stop(); bound=sig; g('ccHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!isSen()); if(!sig){ return; } start(); ping(); },700);
setInterval(ping,240000);
}catch(e){ console.error('Control Center error:',e); }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id), S=v=>String(v==null?'':v);
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const bn=n=>S(n).replace(/\d/g,d=>'০১২৩৪৫৬৭৮৯'[d]), two=n=>bn(String(n).padStart(2,'0'));
const sk=id=>S(id).replace(/[.#$\[\]\/]/g,'_');
const when=ms=>new Date(ms).toLocaleString('bn-BD',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'});
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';

const bigCfg={apiKey:"AIzaSyBNquB4k0OCS0eFWJKuo6GlW4fAcfWhHaI",authDomain:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b.firebaseapp.com",projectId:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b",databaseURL:"https://smart-learning-platform-e3f0b-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com"};
let app=null; for(const a of firebase.apps){ if(a.name==='bigAdmin') app=a; }
if(!app) app=firebase.initializeApp(bigCfg,'bigAdmin');
const bdb=app.database();

const box=document.createElement('div'); box.id='vosBanner';
g('homeSection').children[0].insertAdjacentElement('afterend',box);

let offers={}, legacy=null, off=0, sig='';
bdb.ref('.info/serverTimeOffset').on('value',s=>{ off=s.val()||0; });
bdb.ref('verifyOffers').on('value',s=>{ offers=s.val()||{}; },()=>{});
bdb.ref('verifyOffer').on('value',s=>{ legacy=s.val(); },()=>{});

function eligible(o){
  const r=role(), a=o.audience||'all';
  if(r==='Senior Team Leader'||a==='all') return true;
  if(a==='teams') return !!(o.teamKeys&&o.teamKeys[currentSubAdminKey]);
  if(a==='students'){ const ids=o.studentIds||{}; return (typeof globalStudentsCache!=='undefined'?globalStudentsCache:[]).some(s=>ids[sk(S(s.studentId).toUpperCase())]); }
  return false;
}
const audText=o=>{ const a=o.audience||'all'; return a==='all'?'👥 সব স্টুডেন্টের জন্য':a==='teams'?'🏷 টিম: '+E(o.teamLabel||''):'🎯 নির্দিষ্ট '+bn(Object.keys(o.studentIds||{}).length)+' জন স্টুডেন্টের জন্য'; };

setInterval(()=>{
  const now=Date.now()+off;
  const all=Object.keys(offers).map(k=>Object.assign({id:k},offers[k])); if(legacy) all.push(Object.assign({id:'legacy'},legacy));
  const act=(currentSubAdminKey?all:[]).filter(o=>o.enabled!==false&&o.endMs&&now<o.endMs&&eligible(o)).map(o=>Object.assign(o,{st:now<o.startMs?'up':'live'}))
    .sort((a,b)=>a.st===b.st?a.startMs-b.startMs:(a.st==='live'?-1:1));
  if(!act.length){ box.style.display='none'; box.innerHTML=''; sig=''; return; }
  box.style.display='block';
  const ns=act.map(o=>o.id+'|'+o.updatedAt).join(',');
  if(ns!==sig){
    sig=ns;
    box.innerHTML=act.map(o=>'<div class="vos-c" data-id="'+E(o.id)+'"><div class="vos-h">🎉 '+E(o.title)+'</div><div class="vos-a">'+audText(o)+'</div>'
      +'<span class="vos-tag">'+(o.type==='free'?'🆓 ভেরিফাই সম্পূর্ণ ফ্রি!':'💸 ভেরিফাই ফি মাত্র ৳'+bn(o.fee))+'</span><div class="vos-st"></div>'
      +'<div class="vos-t"><div><b class="vD">০০</b>Days</div><div><b class="vH">০০</b>Hours</div><div><b class="vM">০০</b>Mins</div><div><b class="vS">০০</b>Secs</div></div>'
      +(o.details?'<div class="vos-d">'+E(o.details)+'</div>':'')+(o.image?'<img src="'+E(o.image)+'" alt="">':'')
      +(o.video?'<a class="vos-v" href="'+E(o.video)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">▶ ভিডিও দেখুন</a>':'')
      +'<div class="vos-w">⏱ শুরু: '+E(when(o.startMs))+' | শেষ: '+E(when(o.endMs))+'</div></div>').join('');
  }
  act.forEach(o=>{
    const c=box.querySelector('.vos-c[data-id="'+o.id+'"]'); if(!c) return;
    const left=Math.max(0,Math.floor(((o.st==='up'?o.startMs:o.endMs)-now)/1000));
    c.querySelector('.vos-st').textContent=o.st==='up'?'⏳ অফার শুরু হতে আর বাকি:':'🟢 অফার চলছে — শেষ হতে বাকি:';
    c.querySelector('.vD').textContent=two(Math.floor(left/86400)); c.querySelector('.vH').textContent=two(Math.floor(left%86400/3600));
    c.querySelector('.vM').textContent=two(Math.floor(left%3600/60)); c.querySelector('.vS').textContent=two(left%60);
  });
},1000);
}catch(e){ console.error('Sub-admin verify offer v2 error:',e); }
})();
