<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id), S=v=>String(v==null?'':v);
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const shown=id=>{ const e=g(id); return !!e&&!e.classList.contains('hide'); };
const txt=(sel,def)=>{ const e=document.querySelector(sel); return (e&&e.textContent.trim())||def; };

/* মেনু বাটন (টপ বার-এর বামে) */
const bar=document.querySelector('.top-bar');
const btn=document.createElement('button'); btn.className='mn-btn'; btn.type='button'; btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Menu'); btn.textContent='☰';
bar.insertBefore(btn,bar.firstChild);

/* ড্রয়ার */
const ov=document.createElement('div'); ov.id='mnOv';
const dr=document.createElement('div'); dr.id='mnDr';
document.body.appendChild(ov); document.body.appendChild(dr);

function items(){
  const sen=role()==='Senior Team Leader', a=[];
  a.push(['home','🏠','Home']);
  a.push(['profile','🛡️','My Profile']);
  a.push(['students','👥',txt('#studentsSection .sec-hero h3','Students')]);
  a.push(['balance','💰','Wallet & Pay']);
  a.push(['history','📜','All History']);
  a.push(['withdrawHistory','💸','Withdrawal History']);
  a.push('-');
  if(sen&&g('teamTargetsSection')) a.push(['target','📊','Team Targets']);
  else if(!sen&&g('targetSection')) a.push(['target','🎯','Daily Target']);
  if(shown('navOrders')) a.push(['orders','📦',txt('#homeOrdersLbl','Orders')]);
  if(g('inboxSection')) a.push(['inbox','📬',txt('#inboxLbl','Inbox')]);
  if(shown('ccHomeBox')) a.push(['cc','👑','Control Center']);
  if(shown('snHomeBox')) a.push(['stuNotice','📣','স্টুডেন্ট নোটিশ']);
  a.push('-');
  a.push(['__out','🚪','Logout']);
  return a;
}
function active(){ const s=document.querySelector('.section.active'); return s?s.id.replace(/Section$/,''):'home'; }

function build(){
  const d=currentSubAdminData||{}, cat=role(), ic=(typeof THEMES!=='undefined'&&THEMES[cat])?THEMES[cat].icon:'🎓', cur=active();
  const cur2=(cur==='teamTargets')?'target':cur;
  dr.innerHTML='<div class="mn-head"><div class="mn-av">'+ic+'</div><div><b></b><small></small></div><button class="mn-x" type="button">✕</button></div><div class="mn-list"></div>';
  dr.querySelector('b').textContent=S(d.name)||'Sub-Admin'; dr.querySelector('small').textContent=cat;
  const list=dr.querySelector('.mn-list');
  items().forEach(it=>{
    if(it==='-'){ const s=document.createElement('div'); s.className='mn-sep'; list.appendChild(s); return; }
    const b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.className='mn-it'+(it[0]===cur2?' on':'')+(it[0]==='__out'?' mn-out':'');
    b.innerHTML='<span>'+it[1]+'</span>'; b.appendChild(document.createTextNode(it[2]));
    b.onclick=function(){ close(); if(it[0]==='__out'){ if(confirm('Logout করবেন?')) logout(); } else switchSubTab(it[0]); };
    list.appendChild(b);
  });
  dr.querySelector('.mn-x').onclick=close;
}
function open(){ build(); ov.style.display='block'; requestAnimationFrame(()=>dr.classList.add('open')); }
function close(){ dr.classList.remove('open'); ov.style.display='none'; }
btn.onclick=open; ov.onclick=close;
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='Escape') close(); });
}catch(e){ console.error('Sub-admin menu error:',e); }
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== Sub-Admin অ্যাড-অন: Team Setup + উত্তোলন অনুমোদন চেইন ===== -->

.wf-save{border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 14px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;background:var(--p);cursor:pointer}

<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id), S=v=>String(v==null?'':v), N=v=>Number(v)||0;
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const arr=x=>Array.isArray(x)?x:Object.values(x||{});
const K=()=>currentSubAdminKey;
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isSen=()=>role()==='Senior Team Leader', isTL=()=>role()==='Team Leader', isTr=()=>role()==='Trainer';
const LB={TeamLeader:'Team Leader',Senior:'Senior',Admin:'Admin'};
const when=t=>new Date(N(t)).toLocaleString('en-GB',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'});

/* ---------- UI ---------- */
g('dashboardView').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
<div id="wdApprovalsSection" class="section"><button class="back-btn" onclick="switchSubTab('home')">← ফিরে যান (Back)</button>
 <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">✅</span><div><h3>উত্তোলন অনুমোদন</h3><p id="wdSub"></p></div></div>
 <div class="wf-chips" id="wdChips"></div><div id="wdList"></div></div>
<div id="teamSetSection" class="section"><button class="back-btn" onclick="switchSubTab('home')">← ফিরে যান (Back)</button>
 <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">🧩</span><div><h3>Team Setup</h3><p>কোন Trainer কোন Team Leader-এর আন্ডারে</p></div></div>
 <div id="tsList"></div></div>`);
g('homeSection').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="dash-box hide" id="wdHomeBox" onclick="switchSubTab(\'wdApprovals\')"><div class="ic">✅</div><p id="wdHomeLbl">উত্তোলন অনুমোদন</p></div><div class="dash-box hide" id="tsHomeBox" onclick="switchSubTab(\'teamSet\')"><div class="ic">🧩</div><p>Team Setup</p></div>');
g('homeSection').children[0].insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<div class="wf-alert hide" id="wdAlert" onclick="switchSubTab(\'wdApprovals\')"></div>');
document.querySelector('#balanceSection .tiles').insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<div class="wf-info" id="wfInfo"></div>');
g('pCreated').parentNode.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin','<div class="row hide" id="pTlRow"><span>My Team Leader</span><b id="pTl">-</b></div>');

/* ☰ মেনুতে দুটি অপশন যোগ */
const dr=g('mnDr');
if(dr) new MutationObserver(()=>{
  const l=dr.querySelector('.mn-list'); if(!l||l.querySelector('.wf-mn')) return;
  const out=l.querySelector('.mn-out'); if(!out) return;
  const ref=out.previousElementSibling;
  const add=(t,ic,lb)=>{ const b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.className='mn-it wf-mn'; b.innerHTML='<span>'+ic+'</span>'; b.appendChild(document.createTextNode(lb));
    b.onclick=()=>{ dr.classList.remove('open'); const o=g('mnOv'); if(o) o.style.display='none'; switchSubTab(t); }; l.insertBefore(b,ref); };
  if(isTL()||isSen()) add('wdApprovals','✅','উত্তোলন অনুমোদন');
  if(isSen()) add('teamSet','🧩','Team Setup');
}).observe(dr,{childList:true});

/* ---------- ডাটা ---------- */
let link=null, links={}, staff={}, mineL=[], teamL=[], allL=[];
let refs=[], bound='', wf='wait', prevMine=null, lastWait=null, busy=false;
function on(r,f){ refs.push(r); r.on('value',s=>{ try{ f(s); }catch(e){ console.error(e); } }); }
function stop(){ refs.forEach(r=>{ try{ r.off(); }catch(e){} }); refs=[]; link=null; links={}; staff={}; mineL=[]; teamL=[]; allL=[]; prevMine=null; lastWait=null; }
const rows=s=>{ const a=[]; s.forEach(c=>{ const v=c.val()||{}; v.key=c.key; a.push(v); return false; }); return a; };

/* ---------- রিফান্ড (একবারই, ফ্ল্যাগ দিয়ে সুরক্ষিত) ---------- */
function ensureRefund(id){
  db.ref('subAdminWithdrawals/'+id).transaction(cur=>{ if(!cur||cur.status!=='Rejected'||!cur.chain||cur.refunded) return; cur.refunded=true; return cur; },(err,ok,snap)=>{
    if(err||!ok) return; const w=snap.val();
    db.ref('subAdmins/'+w.subAdminKey+'/balance').transaction(b=>N(b)+N(w.amount));
    db.ref('subAdminTransactions').push({subAdminKey:w.subAdminKey,type:'Withdrawal Refund',amount:w.amount,description:'উত্তোলন Reject — ব্যালেন্স ফেরত',date:new Date().toLocaleString(),timestamp:Date.now()});
  });
}

/* ---------- ট্র্যাকার + কার্ড ---------- */
function track(w){
  const path=arr(w.path).length?arr(w.path):['Admin'], trail=arr(w.trail);
  const steps=['অনুরোধ'].concat(path.map(p=>LB[p])).concat(['সফল']);
  let idx, bad=-1;
  if(w.status==='Approved') idx=steps.length;
  else if(w.status==='Rejected'){ const l=trail[trail.length-1]||{}; bad=1+Math.max(0,path.indexOf(l.stage)); idx=bad; }
  else idx=1+Math.max(0,path.indexOf(w.stage));
  const col=i=>i<idx?'#2ecc71':(i===idx?(bad>=0?'#e74c3c':'#f39c12'):'var(--line)');
  let d=''; steps.forEach((x,i)=>{ d+='<i style="background:'+col(i)+'"></i>'; if(i<steps.length-1) d+='<u style="background:'+(i<idx?'#2ecc71':'var(--line)')+'"></u>'; });
  return '<div class="wf-trk">'+d+'</div><div class="wf-lbl">'+steps.map(x=>'<span>'+E(x)+'</span>').join('')+'</div>';
}
function card(w,act){
  const trail=arr(w.trail), p=w.status==='Pending';
  const oc=w.status==='Approved'?'#2ecc71':w.status==='Rejected'?'#e74c3c':'#f39c12';
  const st=w.status==='Approved'?'✅ উত্তোলন সফল — টাকা পাঠানো হয়েছে':w.status==='Rejected'?'❌ Reject করা হয়েছে — টাকা ব্যালেন্সে ফেরত':'⏳ এখন আছে: '+E(LB[w.stage]||'Admin')+'-এর কাছে';
  const who=act?'<b>'+E(w.name)+'</b> <small>('+E(w.category)+')</small> • ':'';
  return '<div class="wf-c" style="--oc:'+oc+'"><div class="wf-h"><div>'+who+E(w.paymentMethod)+' '+E(w.paymentNumber)+'</div><span class="wf-amt">৳'+E(w.amount)+'</span></div>'
   +'<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">'+E(w.date||'')+'</div>'+track(w)+'<div class="wf-st">'+st+'</div>'
   +trail.map(t=>'<div class="wf-tr">'+(t.action==='Approved'?'✅':'❌')+' <b>'+E(t.by)+'</b> ('+E(t.role)+') '+(t.action==='Approved'?'Approve':'Reject')+' করেছেন • '+E(when(t.ts))+(t.reason?'<br>কারণ: '+E(t.reason):'')+'</div>').join('')
   +(act&&p&&forMe(w)?'<div class="wf-act"><button style="background:#27ae60" data-i="'+E(w.key)+'" onclick="wfDecide(this.dataset.i,true)">✅ Approve</button><button style="background:#e74c3c" data-i="'+E(w.key)+'" onclick="wfDecide(this.dataset.i,false)">❌ Reject</button></div>':'')+'</div>';
}
function forMe(w){
  if(w.status!=='Pending'||w.subAdminKey===K()) return false;
  if(isSen()) return w.stage==='Senior'||w.stage==='TeamLeader';
  if(isTL()) return w.stage==='TeamLeader'&&w.tlKey===K();
  return false;
}
const src=()=>isSen()?allL.filter(w=>w.chain&&w.subAdminKey!==K()):isTL()?teamL:[];

/* ---------- রেন্ডার ---------- */
function renderMine(){
  const box=g('separateWithdrawalHistoryContainer'); if(!box) return;
  const l=mineL.slice().sort((a,b)=>((b.status==='Pending')-(a.status==='Pending'))||N(b.requestedAt)-N(a.requestedAt));
  box.innerHTML=l.length?l.map(w=>w.chain?card(w,false):
    '<div class="hist"><div class="hi">'+(w.status==='Pending'?'🟡':w.status==='Approved'?'✅':'❌')+'</div><div>৳'+E(w.amount)+' via <b>'+E(w.paymentMethod)+'</b> ('+E(w.paymentNumber)+')<br><span>Status: <b>'+E(w.status)+'</b> | '+E(w.date||'N/A')+'</span></div></div>').join(''):"<p class='empty'>এখনো কোনো উইথড্র রিকোয়েস্ট নেই।</p>";
}
function renderApprovals(){
  const box=g('wdList'); if(!box||!(isTL()||isSen())) return;
  const all=src(), wait=all.filter(forMe);
  g('wdChips').innerHTML='<button class="'+(wf==='wait'?'on':'')+'" onclick="wfSet(\'wait\')">⏳ অপেক্ষায় ('+wait.length+')</button><button class="'+(wf==='all'?'on':'')+'" onclick="wfSet(\'all\')">📜 সব প্রক্রিয়া ('+all.length+')</button>';
  g('wdSub').innerText=isSen()?'সব টিমের উত্তোলন ও তাদের বর্তমান ধাপ':'আপনার টিমের Trainer-দের উত্তোলন';
  const l=(wf==='wait'?wait:all).slice().sort((a,b)=>N(b.requestedAt)-N(a.requestedAt));
  box.innerHTML=l.length?l.map(w=>card(w,true)).join(''):"<p class='empty'>কোনো রিকোয়েস্ট নেই।</p>";
  g('wdHomeLbl').textContent='উত্তোলন অনুমোদন'+(wait.length?' ('+wait.length+')':'');
  const a=g('wdAlert');
  if(wait.length){ a.classList.remove('hide'); a.innerHTML='🔔 '+wait.length+'টি উত্তোলন রিকোয়েস্ট আপনার অনুমোদনের অপেক্ষায়<br><small>দেখতে ট্যাপ করুন</small>'; } else a.classList.add('hide');
  if(lastWait!==null&&wait.length>lastWait) toast('🔔 নতুন উত্তোলন রিকোয়েস্ট এসেছে');
  lastWait=wait.length;
}
function renderInfo(){
  const f=g('wfInfo'), r=role(), pr=g('pTlRow');
  const flow=r==='Trainer'?(link?'Team Leader → Senior → Admin':'Senior → Admin'):r==='Team Leader'?'Senior → Admin':'Admin';
  let h='';
  if(r==='Trainer') h=link?'👤 আপনার Team Leader: <b>'+E(link.tlName)+'</b> ('+E(link.tlLoginId)+')<br>':'⚠️ এখনো Team Leader সেট হয়নি, রিকোয়েস্ট সরাসরি Senior-এর কাছে যাবে।<br>';
  f.innerHTML=h+'💸 উত্তোলন যাত্রাপথ: <b>'+flow+'</b>';
  if(pr){ pr.classList.toggle('hide',r!=='Trainer'); g('pTl').textContent=link?link.tlName+' ('+link.tlLoginId+')':'সেট হয়নি'; }
}
function renderTeamSet(){
  const box=g('tsList'); if(!box||!isSen()) return;
  const ae=document.activeElement; if(ae&&box.contains(ae)&&ae.tagName==='SELECT') return;
  const all=Object.values(staff).filter(s=>s.status!=='Suspended');
  const tls=all.filter(s=>s.category==='Team Leader'), trs=all.filter(s=>s.category==='Trainer');
  const cnt={}; trs.forEach(t=>{ const l=links[t.key]; if(l) cnt[l.tlKey]=(cnt[l.tlKey]||0)+1; });
  let h='<div class="wf-info"><b>👥 Team Leader অনুযায়ী Trainer</b><br>'+(tls.map(t=>E(t.name)+': <b>'+(cnt[t.key]||0)+' জন</b>').join('<br>')||'কোনো Team Leader নেই')+'<br>অ্যাসাইন ছাড়া: <b>'+trs.filter(t=>!links[t.key]).length+' জন</b></div>';
  h+=trs.map(t=>{ const cur=links[t.key];
    return '<div class="wf-c" style="--oc:'+(cur?'#2ecc71':'#e74c3c')+'"><b>🎯 '+E(t.name)+'</b> <small>('+E(t.loginId)+')</small><br>বর্তমান: '+(cur?E(cur.tlName):'<span style="color:#e74c3c">কেউ নেই</span>')
     +'<select class="wf-sel"><option value="">— কেউ নেই (সরাসরি Senior) —</option>'+tls.map(x=>'<option value="'+E(x.key)+'"'+(cur&&cur.tlKey===x.key?' selected':'')+'>'+E(x.name)+' ('+E(x.loginId)+')</option>').join('')+'</select>'
     +'<button class="wf-save" data-k="'+E(t.key)+'" onclick="tsSave(this)">💾 সেভ</button></div>'; }).join('')||"<p class='empty'>কোনো Trainer নেই।</p>";
  box.innerHTML=h;
}
function refresh(){ renderMine(); renderApprovals(); renderInfo(); renderTeamSet(); }

/* ---------- অ্যাকশন ---------- */
window.wfSet=function(v){ wf=v; renderApprovals(); };
window.tsSave=function(b){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const tk=b.dataset.k, v=b.parentNode.querySelector('select').value, t=staff[tk], cb=e=>{ if(e) toast('❌ '+e.message,true); else toast('✅ Team সেভ হয়েছে'); };
  if(!t) return;
  if(!v) db.ref('teamStructure/'+tk).remove(cb);
  else { const l=staff[v]; if(!l) return;
    db.ref('teamStructure/'+tk).set({tlKey:v,tlName:S(l.name),tlLoginId:S(l.loginId),trainerName:S(t.name),setBy:S(currentSubAdminData.name),setAt:Date.now()},cb); }
  try{ db.ref('subAdminLogs').push({type:'assign',text:'Team Setup: '+S(t.name)+' → '+(v?S(staff[v].name):'কেউ নেই'),actorKey:K(),actorName:S(currentSubAdminData.name),actorCategory:role(),ts:Date.now()}); }catch(e){}
};
window.wfDecide=function(id,ok){
  const me={key:K(),name:S(currentSubAdminData.name),role:role()};
  let reason='';
  if(ok){ if(!confirm('উত্তোলন Approve করবেন?')) return; }
  else { reason=prompt('Reject-এর কারণ (ঐচ্ছিক):'); if(reason===null) return; }
  db.ref('subAdminWithdrawals/'+id).transaction(cur=>{
    if(!cur||cur.status!=='Pending'||cur.subAdminKey===me.key) return;
    const st=cur.stage, sen=me.role==='Senior Team Leader';
    const okRole=(st==='TeamLeader'&&((me.role==='Team Leader'&&cur.tlKey===me.key)||sen))||(st==='Senior'&&sen);
    if(!okRole) return;
    const trail=arr(cur.trail).slice(); trail.push({stage:st,by:me.name,role:me.role,action:ok?'Approved':'Rejected',reason:S(reason).trim(),ts:Date.now()});
    cur.trail=trail;
    if(ok) cur.stage=(st==='TeamLeader'&&!sen)?'Senior':'Admin';
    else { cur.status='Rejected'; cur.stage='Rejected'; cur.rejectReason=S(reason).trim(); }
    return cur;
  },(err,committed)=>{
    if(err){ toast('❌ '+err.message,true); return; }
    if(!committed){ toast('⚠️ এটি আগেই প্রক্রিয়া হয়েছে বা আপনার অনুমতি নেই',true); return; }
    if(!ok) ensureRefund(id);
    toast(ok?'✅ Approve হয়েছে, পরের ধাপে গেছে':'❌ Reject হয়েছে, টাকা ফেরত যাচ্ছে');
  },false);
};

/* ---------- নতুন উত্তোলন রিকোয়েস্ট ---------- */
window.submitWithdrawal=function(){
  const d=currentSubAdminData; if(!d||busy) return;
  const method=g('wMethod').value, number=g('wNumber').value.trim(), amount=parseFloat(g('wAmount').value);
  if(!number){ toast('❌ Payment Number দিন',true); return; }
  if(isNaN(amount)||amount<=0){ toast('❌ সঠিক পরিমাণ লিখুন',true); return; }
  const k=K(), r=role();
  const tl=(r==='Trainer'&&link)?link:null;
  const path=r==='Trainer'?(tl?['TeamLeader','Senior','Admin']:['Senior','Admin']):r==='Team Leader'?['Senior','Admin']:['Admin'];
  busy=true;
  db.ref('subAdmins/'+k+'/balance').transaction(b=>{ b=N(b); if(amount>b) return; return b-amount; },(err,ok)=>{
    if(err||!ok){ busy=false; toast('❌ Insufficient Balance',true); return; }
    const wRef=db.ref('subAdminWithdrawals').push(), tRef=db.ref('subAdminTransactions').push(), now=Date.now(), date=new Date().toLocaleString(), u={};
    u['subAdminWithdrawals/'+wRef.key]={id:wRef.key,subAdminKey:k,name:S(d.name),loginId:S(d.loginId),category:r,paymentMethod:method,paymentNumber:number,amount,status:'Pending',stage:path[0],path,chain:true,tlKey:tl?tl.tlKey:null,requestedAt:now,date};
    u['subAdminTransactions/'+tRef.key]={id:tRef.key,subAdminKey:k,type:'Withdrawal Request',amount,description:'Requested via '+method+' ('+number+') → '+LB[path[0]],date,timestamp:now};
    db.ref().update(u,e=>{
      busy=false;
      if(e){ db.ref('subAdmins/'+k+'/balance').transaction(b=>N(b)+amount); toast('❌ Failed: '+e.message,true); return; }
      toast('✅ রিকোয়েস্ট জমা হয়েছে — এখন '+LB[path[0]]+'-এর কাছে');
      g('wNumber').value=''; g('wAmount').value=''; if(typeof boom==='function') boom();
    });
  },false);
};

/* ---------- লিসেনার ---------- */
function start(){
  const k=K();
  if(isTr()) on(db.ref('teamStructure/'+k),s=>{ link=s.val(); refresh(); });
  on(db.ref('subAdminWithdrawals').orderByChild('subAdminKey').equalTo(k),s=>{
    mineL=rows(s);
    const cur={}; mineL.forEach(w=>{ cur[w.key]=w.status+'|'+w.stage; if(w.chain&&w.status==='Rejected'&&!w.refunded) ensureRefund(w.key); });
    if(prevMine) mineL.forEach(w=>{ const p=prevMine[w.key], c=cur[w.key]; if(p&&p!==c){
      if(w.status==='Approved') toast('🎉 আপনার উত্তোলন সফল হয়েছে!');
      else if(w.status==='Rejected') toast('❌ আপনার উত্তোলন Reject হয়েছে, টাকা ফেরত এসেছে',true);
      else toast('📤 আপনার উত্তোলন এখন '+(LB[w.stage]||'Admin')+'-এর কাছে'); } });
    prevMine=cur; refresh();
  });
  if(isTL()) on(db.ref('subAdminWithdrawals').orderByChild('tlKey').equalTo(k),s=>{ teamL=rows(s); refresh(); });
  if(isSen()){
    on(db.ref('subAdminWithdrawals').limitToLast(300),s=>{ allL=rows(s); refresh(); });
    on(db.ref('subAdmins'),s=>{ const m={}; s.forEach(c=>{ const v=c.val()||{}; m[c.key]={key:c.key,name:v.name,loginId:v.loginId,category:v.category,status:v.status}; return false; }); staff=m; refresh(); });
    on(db.ref('teamStructure'),s=>{ links=s.val()||{}; refresh(); });
  }
}
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=K(), sig=(k&&currentSubAdminData)?k+'|'+role():'';
  if(sig===bound) return;
  stop(); bound=sig;
  g('wdHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!(isTL()||isSen()));
  g('tsHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!isSen());
  g('wdAlert').classList.add('hide');
  if(!sig) return;
  start(); renderInfo();
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('Withdraw chain add-on error:',e); }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
try{
const S=v=>String(v==null?'':v);
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isSen=()=>role()==='Senior Team Leader';

function bigDb(){
  let app=null; for(const a of firebase.apps){ if(a.name==='bigAdmin') app=a; }
  if(!app) app=firebase.initializeApp({apiKey:"AIzaSyBNquB4k0OCS0eFWJKuo6GlW4fAcfWhHaI",authDomain:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b.firebaseapp.com",projectId:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b",databaseURL:"https://smart-learning-platform-e3f0b-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com"},'bigAdmin');
  return app.database();
}

let refMap={}, uMap={}, rRef=null, uRef=null, sig='';

function getReferrer(id){
  const u=uMap[S(id).toUpperCase()]||{};
  const rid=refMap[S(id).toUpperCase()]||u.by||'';
  if(!rid) return null;
  const ru=uMap[S(rid).toUpperCase()]||{};
  return {id:rid,name:ru.name||'',phone:ru.phone||''};
}

function enhance(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('#studentsContainer .student-card').forEach(c=>{
    if(c.querySelector('.rf-x')) return;
    let id='', anchor=null;
    c.querySelectorAll('p').forEach(p=>{
      const b=p.querySelector('b'); if(!b) return;
      const l=b.textContent.trim();
      if(l==='ID:') id=p.textContent.replace(b.textContent,'').trim();
      if(l==='Mobile:') anchor=p;
    });
    if(!id||!anchor) return;
    const r=getReferrer(id);
    const h=r
      ? '<div class="rf-x">🤝 <b>Referred By:</b> <b class="rn">'+E(r.name||'Unknown')+'</b> <small>('+E(r.id)+')</small>'+(r.phone?' | 📞 '+E(r.phone):'')+'</div>'
      : '<div class="rf-x no">🏢 <b>কোম্পানির রেফার</b></div>';
    anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',h);
  });
}

/* আগের renderStudents-এর পরে Referred By যোগ */
const _rs=window.renderStudents;
window.renderStudents=function(){ _rs.apply(this,arguments); try{enhance();}catch(e){console.error(e);} };

/* লিসেনার (লগইন/লগআউটে অটো) */
function stop(){
  if(rRef){ try{rRef.off();}catch(e){} rRef=null; }
  if(uRef){ try{uRef.off();}catch(e){} uRef=null; }
  refMap={}; uMap={};
}
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, s=(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isSen())?k+'|'+role():'';
  if(s===sig) return;
  stop(); sig=s;
  if(!s) return;
  const bd=bigDb();
  rRef=bd.ref('referrals');
  rRef.on('value',snap=>{
    const m={};
    snap.forEach(c=>{ const r=c.val()||{}; if(r.newStudentId&&r.referrerId) m[S(r.newStudentId).toUpperCase()]=r.referrerId; return false; });
    refMap=m;
    if(typeof window.renderStudents==='function') window.renderStudents();
  },()=>{});
  uRef=bd.ref('users');
  uRef.on('value',snap=>{
    const m={};
    snap.forEach(c=>{ const u=c.val()||{}; if(u.id) m[S(u.id).toUpperCase()]={name:u.name,phone:u.phone,by:u.referredBy||u.referrerId||''}; return false; });
    uMap=m;
    if(typeof window.renderStudents==='function') window.renderStudents();
  },()=>{});
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('Referred-by (sub-admin) error:',e); }
})();
</script>

/* ---------- ১) সব সাব-অ্যাডমিনের দৈনিক লগইন রেকর্ড ---------- */
function track(){
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, d=currentSubAdminData; if(!k||!d) return;
  const now=Date.now(), r=db.ref('subAdminDaily/'+dk()+'/'+k);
  r.child('first').transaction(v=>v||now);
  r.update({name:S(d.name),loginId:S(d.loginId),category:S(d.category),last:now});
}
let trKey='';

let date=dk(), users=[], sDaily={}, staff={}, act={}, sdaily={}, sf='in', tf='all', q='', shown=80;
let refs=[], sig='';
g('lrDate').value=date;
g('lrDate').addEventListener('change',e=>{ date=e.target.value||dk(); });
g('lrQ').addEventListener('input',e=>{ q=e.target.value.toLowerCase().trim(); shown=80; render(); });
window.lrSf=function(v){ sf=v; shown=80; render(); };
window.lrTf=function(v){ tf=v; render(); };
window.lrMore=function(){ shown+=80; render(); };

function staffRows(){
  return Object.keys(staff).map(k=>staff[k]).filter(s=>(s.category==='Team Leader'||s.category==='Trainer')&&s.status!=='Suspended').map(s=>{
    const d=sdaily[s.key]||{}, a=act[s.key]||{};
    let last=N(d.last), first=N(d.first);
    if(!last&&a.lastSeen&&dOf(N(a.lastSeen))===date){ last=N(a.lastSeen); }
    return Object.assign({},s,{first,last,inn:!!last});
  }).sort((a,b)=>(b.inn-a.inn)||S(a.name).localeCompare(S(b.name)));
}
function stuRows(){
  return users.map(u=>{
    const r=sDaily[u.key]||{}; let last=N(r.last), first=N(r.first);
    if(!last&&u.lastSeen&&dOf(N(u.lastSeen))===date) last=N(u.lastSeen);
    return Object.assign({},u,{first,last,inn:!!last});
  });
}
const line=x=>x.inn?'✅ লগইন '+(x.first?'প্রথম '+tm(x.first)+' • ':'')+'শেষ '+tm(x.last):'❌ লগইন করেনি';

function render(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const st=staffRows(), su=stuRows();
  const sIn=st.filter(x=>x.inn).length, uIn=su.filter(x=>x.inn).length;
  g('lrSum').innerHTML='<div><b>'+uIn+'</b>✅ স্টুডেন্ট লগইন</div><div><b>'+(su.length-uIn)+'</b>❌ স্টুডেন্ট লগইন করেনি</div><div><b>'+sIn+'</b>✅ টিম লগইন</div><div><b>'+(st.length-sIn)+'</b>❌ টিম লগইন করেনি</div>';
  const ch=(fn,cur,a)=>a.map(x=>'<button class="'+(cur===x[0]?'on':'')+'" onclick="'+fn+'(\''+x[0]+'\')">'+x[1]+'</button>').join('');
  g('lrStChips').innerHTML=ch('lrTf',tf,[['all','সব ('+st.length+')'],['in','✅ লগইন ('+sIn+')'],['out','❌ করেনি ('+(st.length-sIn)+')']]);
  g('lrSuChips').innerHTML=ch('lrSf',sf,[['in','✅ লগইন ('+uIn+')'],['out','❌ করেনি ('+(su.length-uIn)+')'],['all','সব ('+su.length+')']]);
  const fs=st.filter(x=>tf==='all'||(tf==='in')===x.inn);
  g('lrStaff').innerHTML=fs.map(x=>'<div class="lr-c '+(x.inn?'':'out')+'"><b>'+E(x.name)+'</b> <span class="lr-s">'+E(x.category)+' • '+E(x.loginId)+'</span><br>'+line(x)+'</div>').join('')||"<p class='empty'>কেউ নেই।</p>";
  const fu=su.filter(x=>(sf==='all'||(sf==='in')===x.inn)&&(!q||(S(x.name)+S(x.id)+S(x.phone)).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1)).sort((a,b)=>N(b.last)-N(a.last));
  g('lrStu').innerHTML=fu.length
    ? fu.slice(0,shown).map(x=>'<div class="lr-c '+(x.inn?'':'out')+'"><b>'+E(x.name)+'</b> <span class="lr-s">'+E(x.id)+' • '+E(x.phone)+'</span><br>'+line(x)+'</div>').join('')
      +(fu.length>shown?'<div style="text-align:center"><button class="back-btn" onclick="lrMore()">আরও দেখুন ('+(fu.length-shown)+')</button></div>':'')
    : "<p class='empty'>কেউ নেই।</p>";
}

/* ---------- ৪) লিসেনার ---------- */
function on(r,f){ refs.push(r); r.on('value',s=>{ try{ f(s); }catch(e){ console.error(e); } },e=>console.error(e)); }
function stop(){ refs.forEach(r=>{ try{ r.off(); }catch(e){} }); refs=[]; users=[]; sDaily={}; staff={}; act={}; sdaily={}; }
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, s=(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isSen())?k+'|'+date:'';
  g('lrHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isSen()));
  if(s===sig) return;
  stop(); sig=s; if(!s) return;
  const bd=bigDb();
  on(bd.ref('users'),sn=>{ const a=[]; sn.forEach(c=>{ const u=c.val()||{}; a.push({key:c.key,id:u.id,name:u.name,phone:u.phone,lastSeen:u.lastSeen}); return false; }); users=a; render(); });
  on(bd.ref('daily_logins/'+date),sn=>{ sDaily=sn.val()||{}; render(); });
  on(db.ref('subAdmins'),sn=>{ const m={}; sn.forEach(c=>{ const v=c.val()||{}; m[c.key]={key:c.key,name:v.name,loginId:v.loginId,category:v.category,status:v.status}; return false; }); staff=m; render(); });
  on(db.ref('subAdminActivity'),sn=>{ act=sn.val()||{}; render(); });
  on(db.ref('subAdminDaily/'+date),sn=>{ sdaily=sn.val()||{}; render(); });
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('Login report error:',e); }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id),S=v=>String(v==null?'':v),N=v=>Number(v)||0;
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isSen=()=>role()==='Senior Team Leader';
const dOf=t=>new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka'}), dk=()=>dOf(Date.now());
const dayStart=d=>Date.parse(d+'T00:00:00+06:00'), dayEnd=d=>dayStart(d)+864e5-1;
const tm=t=>new Date(N(t)).toLocaleTimeString('en-US',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
const PC='-0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ_abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
function keyTs(k){ k=S(k); if(k.length!==20) return 0; let t=0; for(let i=0;i<8;i++){ const x=PC.indexOf(k[i]); if(x<0) return 0; t=t*64+x; } return t; }
function refTs(r){ const n=N(r.timestamp)||N(r.createdAt)||N(r.ts); if(n) return n; const k=keyTs(r.key); if(k>1e12) return k; const p=Date.parse(r.date); return isNaN(p)?0:p; }
function bigDb(){
  let app=null; for(const a of firebase.apps){ if(a.name==='bigAdmin') app=a; }
  if(!app) app=firebase.initializeApp({apiKey:"AIzaSyBNquB4k0OCS0eFWJKuo6GlW4fAcfWhHaI",authDomain:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b.firebaseapp.com",projectId:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b",databaseURL:"https://smart-learning-platform-e3f0b-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com"},'bigAdmin');
  return app.database();
}

 <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">📅</span><div><h3>দৈনিক রেফার ও সেল</h3><p>কে কতজনকে রেফার করেছে ও কে কয়টি প্রোডাক্ট বিক্রি করেছে</p></div></div>
 <div class="card"><div class="form-group" style="margin:0"><label>📅 তারিখ (ঢাকা সময়)</label><input type="date" id="drDate"></div></div>
 <div class="dr-h">🤝 রেফার রিপোর্ট</div><div id="drRefSum"></div><div id="drRef"></div>
 <div class="dr-h">🛒 প্রোডাক্ট সেল রিপোর্ট</div><div id="drSellSum"></div><div id="drSell"></div>

const dr=g('mnDr');
if(dr) new MutationObserver(()=>{
  const l=dr.querySelector('.mn-list'); if(!l||l.querySelector('.dr-mn')||!isSen()) return;
  const out=l.querySelector('.mn-out'); if(!out) return;
  const b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.className='mn-it dr-mn'; b.innerHTML='<span>📅</span>'; b.appendChild(document.createTextNode('দৈনিক রেফার ও সেল'));
  b.onclick=()=>{ dr.classList.remove('open'); const o=g('mnOv'); if(o) o.style.display='none'; switchSubTab('dailyRep'); };
  l.insertBefore(b,out.previousElementSibling);
}).observe(dr,{childList:true});

let date=dk(), refs=[], orders=[], umap={}, hs=[], sig='';
g('drDate').value=date;
g('drDate').addEventListener('change',e=>{ date=e.target.value||dk(); });
const nameOf=id=>S((umap[S(id).toUpperCase()]||{}).name);
const tiles=a=>'<div class="dr-t">'+a.map(x=>'<div><b>'+x[0]+'</b>'+x[1]+'</div>').join('')+'</div>';

function render(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const s0=dayStart(date), e0=dayEnd(date);
  const today=refs.filter(r=>{ const t=refTs(r); return t>=s0&&t<=e0; });
  const by={}; today.forEach(r=>{ const k=S(r.referrerId); (by[k]=by[k]||[]).push(r); });
  const rows=Object.keys(by).map(k=>({id:k,list:by[k]})).sort((a,b)=>b.list.length-a.list.length);
  const cnt=st=>today.filter(r=>r.status===st).length;
  g('drRefSum').innerHTML=tiles([[today.length,'মোট রেফার'],[rows.length,'রেফারার'],[cnt('Pending'),'⏳ Pending'],[cnt('Approved'),'✅ Approved'],[cnt('Rejected'),'❌ Rejected'],[refs.length,'সব সময়ের রেফার']]);
  g('drRef').innerHTML=rows.map(r=>'<div class="dr-c"><b>'+E(nameOf(r.id)||'Unknown')+'</b> <span class="dr-s">('+E(r.id)+')</span><span class="dr-b">'+r.list.length+' জন</span>'
    +r.list.map(x=>'<div class="dr-m">• '+E(x.newStudentName)+' <span class="dr-s">('+E(x.newStudentId)+')</span> — '+E(x.status||'Pending')+(refTs(x)?' • '+tm(refTs(x)):'')+'</div>').join('')+'</div>').join('')||"<p class='empty'>এই তারিখে কোনো রেফার হয়নি।</p>";
  const all={}; refs.forEach(r=>{ if(r.referrerId) all[r.referrerId]=(all[r.referrerId]||0)+1; });
  g('drTop').innerHTML=Object.keys(all).sort((a,b)=>all[b]-all[a]).slice(0,10).map((k,i)=>'<div class="dr-c"><b>#'+(i+1)+' '+E(nameOf(k)||'Unknown')+'</b> <span class="dr-s">('+E(k)+')</span><span class="dr-b">'+all[k]+'</span></div>').join('')||"<p class='empty'>ডাটা নেই।</p>";

  const live=orders.filter(o=>o&&o.status!=='Cancelled'), canc=orders.length-live.length;
  const val=o=>N(o.soldTotal!=null?o.soldTotal:o.soldPrice);
  const sel={};
  live.forEach(o=>{ const k=S(o.studentId); const x=sel[k]=sel[k]||{id:k,orders:0,qty:0,val:0,del:0,items:{}}; const q=N(o.qty)||1; x.orders++; x.qty+=q; x.val+=val(o); if(o.status==='Delivered') x.del++; const p=S(o.productName); x.items[p]=(x.items[p]||0)+q; });
  const sr=Object.values(sel).sort((a,b)=>b.orders-a.orders);
  g('drSellSum').innerHTML=tiles([[live.length,'মোট অর্ডার'],[live.reduce((a,o)=>a+(N(o.qty)||1),0),'মোট পিস'],[sr.length,'সেলার'],[live.filter(o=>o.status==='Delivered').length,'🟢 Delivered'],['৳'+live.reduce((a,o)=>a+val(o),0),'বিক্রয় মূল্য'],[canc,'❌ Cancelled']]);
  g('drSell').innerHTML=sr.map(x=>'<div class="dr-c s"><b>'+E(nameOf(x.id)||'Unknown')+'</b> <span class="dr-s">('+E(x.id)+')</span><span class="dr-b">'+x.orders+' অর্ডার</span>'
    +'<div class="dr-m">মোট '+x.qty+' পিস • Delivered '+x.del+' • বিক্রয় ৳'+x.val+'</div>'
    +'<div class="dr-m">'+Object.keys(x.items).map(p=>'📦 '+E(p)+' × '+x.items[p]).join('<br>')+'</div></div>').join('')||"<p class='empty'>এই তারিখে কোনো প্রোডাক্ট সেল হয়নি।</p>";

  const hc=g('drHomeCard'); hc.classList.remove('hide');
  hc.innerHTML='📅 <b>'+(date===dk()?'আজকের':E(date))+' হিসাব</b><br>🤝 রেফার: <b>'+today.length+'</b> জন ('+rows.length+' জন রেফারার)<br>🛒 প্রোডাক্ট সেল: <b>'+live.length+'</b> অর্ডার ('+sr.length+' জন সেলার)<br><small style="color:var(--mute)">বিস্তারিত দেখতে ট্যাপ করুন</small>';
}

function stop(){ hs.forEach(r=>{ try{ r.off(); }catch(e){} }); hs=[]; refs=[]; orders=[]; umap={}; }
function on(r,f,er){ hs.push(r); r.on('value',s=>{ try{ f(s); }catch(e){ console.error(e); } },er||(e=>console.error(e))); }
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, ok=!!(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isSen()), s=ok?k+'|'+date:'';
  g('drHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!ok);
  if(!ok) g('drHomeCard').classList.add('hide');
  if(s===sig) return;
  stop(); sig=s; if(!s) return;
  const bd=bigDb();
  on(bd.ref('referrals'),sn=>{ const a=[]; sn.forEach(c=>{ a.push(Object.assign({key:c.key},c.val())); return false; }); refs=a; render(); });
  on(bd.ref('users'),sn=>{ const m={}; sn.forEach(c=>{ const u=c.val()||{}; if(u.id) m[S(u.id).toUpperCase()]={name:u.name}; return false; }); umap=m; render(); });
  on(db.ref('smart_product_orders').orderByChild('timestamp').startAt(dayStart(date)).endAt(dayEnd(date)),sn=>{ const a=[]; sn.forEach(c=>{ a.push(Object.assign({key:c.key},c.val())); return false; }); orders=a; render(); },
    e=>{ g('drSell').innerHTML="<p class='empty' style='color:#c0392b'>❌ অর্ডার লোড হয়নি: "+E(e.message)+"</p>"; });
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('Daily refer/sell (sub-admin) error:',e); }
})();
</script>

let orders=[], umap={}, f='All', shown=40, hs=[], sig='';
const nameOf=id=>S((umap[S(id).toUpperCase()]||{}).name);
g('ssQ').addEventListener('input',()=>{ shown=40; render(); });
window.ssF=v=>{ f=v; shown=40; render(); };
window.ssMore=()=>{ shown+=40; render(); };

function render(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const q=S(g('ssQ').value).toLowerCase().trim();
  const cnt={All:orders.length}; orders.forEach(o=>{ const s=o.status||'Pending'; cnt[s]=(cnt[s]||0)+1; });
  g('ssChips').innerHTML=['All'].concat(KEYS).map(k=>'<button class="'+(f===k?'on':'')+'" onclick="ssF(\''+k+'\')">'+(k==='All'?'সব':ST[k][0])+' ('+(cnt[k]||0)+')</button>').join('');
  const by={};
  orders.forEach(o=>{
    const s=o.status||'Pending'; if(f!=='All'&&s!==f) return;
    if(q&&![o.studentId,nameOf(o.studentId),o.productName,o.orderId,o.name,o.phone].some(v=>S(v).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1)) return;
    (by[S(o.studentId)]=by[S(o.studentId)]||[]).push(o);
  });
  const rows=Object.keys(by).map(k=>({id:k,list:by[k].sort((a,b)=>N(b.timestamp)-N(a.timestamp))})).sort((a,b)=>b.list.length-a.list.length);
  g('ssSum').innerHTML='<div class="ss-t"><div><b>'+rows.length+'</b>সেলার স্টুডেন্ট</div><div><b>'+rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.list.length,0)+'</b>মোট অর্ডার</div><div><b>'+rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.list.reduce((x,o)=>x+(N(o.qty)||1),0),0)+'</b>মোট পিস</div></div>';
  g('ssList').innerHTML=(rows.slice(0,shown).map(r=>{
    const sc={}; r.list.forEach(o=>{ const s=o.status||'Pending'; sc[s]=(sc[s]||0)+1; });
    return '<div class="ss-c"><b>'+E(nameOf(r.id)||'Unknown')+'</b> <span class="ss-s">('+E(r.id)+')</span><span class="ss-b">'+r.list.length+' টি</span>'
     +'<div style="margin-top:4px;font-size:12px">'+KEYS.filter(k=>sc[k]).map(k=>ST[k][0]+' '+sc[k]).join(' • ')+'</div>'
     +r.list.map(o=>{ const st=ST[o.status||'Pending']||[o.status,'#999'];
       return '<div class="ss-o" style="--oc:'+st[1]+'"><b>📦 '+E(o.productName)+'</b> × '+(N(o.qty)||1)+' <span class="ss-st" style="background:'+st[1]+'">'+st[0]+'</span><br><span class="ss-s">'+E(o.orderId)+' • '+E(o.date||'')+' • কাস্টমার: '+E(o.name)+' ('+E(o.phone)+')</span></div>'; }).join('')+'</div>';
  }).join('')||"<p class='empty'>কোনো অর্ডার পাওয়া যায়নি।</p>")
  +(rows.length>shown?'<div style="text-align:center"><button class="back-btn" onclick="ssMore()">আরও দেখুন ('+(rows.length-shown)+')</button></div>':'');
}
function stop(){ hs.forEach(r=>{ try{ r.off(); }catch(e){} }); hs=[]; orders=[]; umap={}; }
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, ok=!!(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isSen()), s=ok?k:'';
  g('ssHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!ok);
  if(s===sig) return;
  stop(); sig=s; if(!s) return;
  const r1=db.ref('smart_product_orders').orderByChild('timestamp').limitToLast(1000); hs.push(r1);
  r1.on('value',sn=>{ const a=[]; sn.forEach(c=>{ a.push(Object.assign({key:c.key},c.val())); return false; }); orders=a; render(); },
    e=>{ g('ssList').innerHTML="<p class='empty' style='color:#c0392b'>❌ অর্ডার লোড হয়নি: "+E(e.message)+"</p>"; });
  const r2=bigDb().ref('users'); hs.push(r2);
  r2.on('value',sn=>{ const m={}; sn.forEach(c=>{ const u=c.val()||{}; if(u.id) m[S(u.id).toUpperCase()]={name:u.name}; return false; }); umap=m; render(); },()=>{});
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('Student sales (sub-admin) error:',e); }
})();

let orders={}, attached={}, idxRef=null, f='All', shown=40, sig='';
function nameOf(id){
  const c=(typeof globalStudentsCache!=='undefined'?globalStudentsCache:[]).find(s=>S(s.studentId).toUpperCase()===S(id).toUpperCase());
  return c?S(c.name):'';
}
g('tmQ').addEventListener('input',()=>{ shown=40; render(); });
window.tmF=v=>{ f=v; shown=40; render(); };
window.tmMore=()=>{ shown+=40; render(); };

function render(){
  if(!isTL()) return;
  const all=Object.keys(orders).map(k=>orders[k]).filter(o=>o&&o.teamKeys&&o.teamKeys[currentSubAdminKey]);
  const cnt={All:all.length}; all.forEach(o=>{ const s=o.status||'Pending'; cnt[s]=(cnt[s]||0)+1; });
  g('tmChips').innerHTML=['All'].concat(KEYS).map(k=>'<button class="'+(f===k?'on':'')+'" onclick="tmF(\''+k+'\')">'+(k==='All'?'সব':ST[k][0])+' ('+(cnt[k]||0)+')</button>').join('');
  const q=S(g('tmQ').value).toLowerCase().trim(), by={};
  all.forEach(o=>{
    const s=o.status||'Pending'; if(f!=='All'&&s!==f) return;
    if(q&&![o.studentId,nameOf(o.studentId),o.studentName,o.productName,o.orderId,o.name,o.phone].some(v=>S(v).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1)) return;
    (by[S(o.studentId)]=by[S(o.studentId)]||[]).push(o);
  });
  const rows=Object.keys(by).map(k=>({id:k,list:by[k].sort((a,b)=>N(b.timestamp)-N(a.timestamp))})).sort((a,b)=>b.list.length-a.list.length);
  const act=all.filter(o=>o.status!=='Cancelled'&&o.status!=='Returned').length;
  g('tmSum').innerHTML='<div class="tm-t"><div><b>'+all.length+'</b>মোট অর্ডার</div><div><b>'+(cnt.Delivered||0)+'</b>🟢 Delivered</div><div><b>'+(cnt.TeamReview||0)+'</b>🕐 রিভিউ বাকি</div><div><b>'+rows.length+'</b>সেলার স্টুডেন্ট</div><div><b>'+act+'</b>চলমান</div><div><b>'+((cnt.Cancelled||0)+(cnt.Returned||0))+'</b>Cancel/Return</div></div>';
  g('tmList').innerHTML=(rows.slice(0,shown).map(r=>{
    const sc={}; r.list.forEach(o=>{ const s=o.status||'Pending'; sc[s]=(sc[s]||0)+1; });
    const nm=nameOf(r.id)||S(r.list[0].studentName)||'Unknown';
    return '<div class="tm-c"><b>'+E(nm)+'</b> <span class="tm-s">('+E(r.id)+')</span><span class="tm-b">'+r.list.length+' টি</span>'
     +'<div style="margin-top:4px;font-size:12px">'+KEYS.filter(k=>sc[k]).map(k=>ST[k][0]+' '+sc[k]).join(' • ')+'</div>'
     +r.list.map(o=>{ const st=ST[o.status||'Pending']||[o.status,'#999'];
       return '<div class="tm-o" style="--oc:'+st[1]+'"><b>📦 '+E(o.productName)+'</b> × '+(N(o.qty)||1)+' <span class="tm-st" style="background:'+st[1]+'">'+st[0]+'</span><br><span class="tm-s">'+E(o.orderId)+' • '+E(o.date||'')+' • কাস্টমার: '+E(o.name)+' ('+E(o.phone)+')</span></div>'; }).join('')+'</div>';
  }).join('')||"<p class='empty'>কোনো অর্ডার পাওয়া যায়নি।</p>")
  +(rows.length>shown?'<div style="text-align:center"><button class="back-btn" onclick="tmMore()">আরও দেখুন ('+(rows.length-shown)+')</button></div>':'');
  g('tmHomeLbl').textContent='টিমের সেল ও ধাপ'+(all.length?' ('+all.length+')':'');
}

function stop(){
  Object.keys(attached).forEach(id=>{ try{ attached[id].off(); }catch(e){} });
  attached={}; orders={};
  if(idxRef){ try{ idxRef.off(); }catch(e){} idxRef=null; }
}
function start(key){
  idxRef=db.ref('team_order_index/'+key);
  idxRef.on('value',snap=>{
    const ids={}; snap.forEach(c=>{ ids[c.key]=true; return false; });
    Object.keys(attached).forEach(id=>{ if(!ids[id]){ try{ attached[id].off(); }catch(e){} delete attached[id]; delete orders[id]; } });
    Object.keys(ids).forEach(id=>{
      if(attached[id]) return;
      const r=db.ref('smart_product_orders/'+id); attached[id]=r;
      r.on('value',s=>{ if(s.exists()) orders[id]=s.val(); else delete orders[id]; render(); });
    });
    render();
  },e=>{ g('tmList').innerHTML="<p class='empty' style='color:#c0392b'>❌ লোড হয়নি: "+E(e.message)+"</p>"; });
}
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, ok=!!(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isTL()), s=ok?k:'';
  g('tmHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!ok);
  if(s===sig) return;
  stop(); sig=s; if(s) start(s);
},700);
setInterval(render,5000);
}catch(e){ console.error('Team sales (Team Leader) error:',e); }
})();
</script>

<script>

function keyTs(k){ k=S(k); if(k.length!==20) return 0; let t=0; for(let i=0;i<8;i++){ const x=PC.indexOf(k[i]); if(x<0) return 0; t=t*64+x; } return t; }
function refTs(r){ const n=N(r.timestamp)||N(r.createdAt)||N(r.ts); if(n) return n; const k=keyTs(r.key); if(k>1e12) return k; const p=Date.parse(r.date); return isNaN(p)?0:p; }

  if(!app) app=firebase.initializeApp({apiKey:"AIzaSyBNquB4k0OCS0eFWJKuo6GlW4fAcfWhHaI",authDomain:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b.firebaseapp.com",projectId:"smart-learning-platform-e3f0b",databaseURL:"https://smart-learning-platform-e3f0b-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com"},'bigAdmin');
  return app.database();
}

function logLines(l){
  if(!l) return '';
  const cs=(l.commissions||[]).map(c=>E(c.name)+' ('+(c.category==='Team Leader'?'TL':'Trainer')+' '+c.rate+'%) ৳'+c.amount).join(', ');
  return '<div class="rfx-box2">💰 রেফারারকে দেওয়া: <b>৳'+E(l.amount)+'</b><br>👑 Senior বোনাস ('+E(l.seniorBonus&&l.seniorBonus.rate)+'%): <b>৳'+E(l.seniorBonus&&l.seniorBonus.amount)+'</b> — '+E(l.seniorName)+'<br>👔 টিম কমিশন: '+(cs||'দেওয়া হয়নি')+'<br>👥 নতুন স্টুডেন্ট অ্যাসাইন: '+((l.assignedTo||[]).map(a=>E(a.name)+' ('+(a.category==='Team Leader'?'TL':'Trainer')+')').join(', ')||'দেওয়া হয়নি')+'</div>';
}

function render(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const ae=document.activeElement; if(ae&&ae.classList&&ae.classList.contains('rfx-in')&&ae.id!=='rfxQ'&&g('rfxList').contains(ae)) return;

  const mine=logs.filter(l=>l.seniorKey===m), ap=mine.filter(l=>l.action==='Approved'), rj=mine.filter(l=>l.action==='Rejected');
  const sum=(a,f)=>a.reduce((s,x)=>s+N(f(x)),0);
  const cnt={Pending:0,Approved:0,Rejected:0}; refs.forEach(r=>{ const s=r.status||'Pending'; if(cnt[s]!==undefined) cnt[s]++; });
  g('rfxSum').innerHTML='<div class="rfx-t"><div><b>'+cnt.Pending+'</b>⏳ Pending</div><div><b>'+ap.length+'</b>✅ আমি Approve</div><div><b>'+rj.length+'</b>❌ আমি Reject</div>'
    +'<div><b>৳'+sum(ap,x=>x.amount)+'</b>রেফারারদের দিয়েছি</div><div><b>৳'+sum(ap,x=>x.commissionTotal)+'</b>টিমকে কমিশন</div><div><b>৳'+sum(ap,x=>x.seniorBonus&&x.seniorBonus.amount)+'</b>👑 আমার বোনাস</div></div>';
  g('rfxChips').innerHTML=[['Pending','⏳ Pending'],['Approved','✅ Approved'],['Rejected','❌ Rejected']].map(x=>'<button class="'+(tab===x[0]?'on':'')+'" onclick="rfxTab(\''+x[0]+'\')">'+x[1]+' ('+cnt[x[0]]+')</button>').join('');
  const list=refs.filter(r=>(r.status||'Pending')===tab&&(!q||[r.referrerId,nameOf(r.referrerId),r.newStudentId,r.newStudentName].some(v=>S(v).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1))).sort((a,b)=>refTs(b)-refTs(a));

  g('rfxList').innerHTML=(list.slice(0,shown).map(r=>{
    const nm=nameOf(r.referrerId)||'Unknown', t=refTs(r);
    const head='<b>🤝 '+E(nm)+'</b> <span class="rfx-s">('+E(r.referrerId)+')</span><br>🆕 নতুন মেম্বার: <b>'+E(r.newStudentName)+'</b> <span class="rfx-s">('+E(r.newStudentId)+')</span><br><span class="rfx-s">'+E(r.date||(t?when(t):''))+'</span>';
    if(tab==='Pending') return '<div class="rfx-c" style="--oc:#f39c12">'+head
      +'<input class="rfx-in" type="number" min="1" data-k="'+E(r.key)+'" value="'+E(amts[r.key]||'')+'" placeholder="রেফারারকে কত টাকা দিবেন (৳)" oninput="rfxAmt(this)">'
      +'<div class="rfx-act"><button style="background:#27ae60" data-k="'+E(r.key)+'" onclick="rfxStart(this.dataset.k)">✅ Approve & Pay</button><button style="background:#e74c3c" data-k="'+E(r.key)+'" onclick="rfxReject(this.dataset.k)">❌ Reject</button></div></div>';
    if(tab==='Approved'){ const by=r.approvedBy&&r.approvedBy.name; return '<div class="rfx-c" style="--oc:#2ecc71">'+head+'<br>✅ Approved'+(r.reward!=null?' • ৳'+E(r.reward):'')+' • '+(by?E(by):'Admin/অন্য')+logLines(logBy[r.key])+'</div>'; }
    const by=r.rejectedBy&&r.rejectedBy.name; return '<div class="rfx-c" style="--oc:#e74c3c">'+head+'<br>❌ Rejected'+(by?' • '+E(by):'')+(r.rejectReason?'<br>কারণ: '+E(r.rejectReason):'')+'</div>';
  }).join('')||"<p class='empty'>কোনো রেফার নেই।</p>")
  +(list.length>shown?'<div style="text-align:center"><button class="back-btn" onclick="rfxMore()">আরও দেখুন ('+(list.length-shown)+')</button></div>':'');
}

/* ---------- Approve ডায়ালগ ---------- */
function closeM(){ g('rfxOv').style.display='none'; g('rfxBox').innerHTML=''; cur=null; }
window.rfxClose=closeM;
g('rfxOv').addEventListener('click',e=>{ if(e.target===g('rfxOv')&&!busy) closeM(); });

window.rfxStart=function(k){
  if(!isSen()||busy) return;
  const r=refs.find(x=>x.key===k); if(!r||(r.status||'Pending')!=='Pending'){ toast('⚠️ এটি আগেই প্রক্রিয়া হয়েছে',true); return; }
  const amt=parseFloat(amts[k]); if(!(amt>0)){ toast('❌ রেফারারকে কত টাকা দিবেন লিখুন',true); return; }
  const ref=umap[S(r.referrerId).toUpperCase()]; if(!ref){ toast('❌ Referrer ID সিস্টেমে পাওয়া যায়নি',true); return; }
  g('rfxOv').style.display='flex'; g('rfxBox').innerHTML='<p style="text-align:center;padding:20px">⏳ টিম লোড হচ্ছে...</p>';
  Promise.all([db.ref('studentAssignments/'+sk(r.referrerId)).once('value'),db.ref('studentAssignments/'+sk(r.newStudentId)).once('value')]).then(res=>{
    const asg=res[0].val()||{}, already=res[1].val()||{}, team=[];
    Object.keys(asg).forEach(ak=>{ const sa=staff[ak]; if(sa&&(sa.category==='Team Leader'||sa.category==='Trainer')&&sa.status!=='Suspended') team.push({key:ak,name:sa.name,category:sa.category}); });
    /* নতুন স্টুডেন্টকে দেওয়ার জন্য সব Team Leader ও Trainer */
    const all=Object.keys(staff).map(x=>staff[x]).filter(x=>(x.category==='Team Leader'||x.category==='Trainer')&&x.status!=='Suspended')
      .sort((a,b)=>S(a.category).localeCompare(S(b.category))||S(a.name).localeCompare(S(b.name)));
    cur={k,r,amt,ref,team,all,already}; drawM();
  }).catch(e=>{ closeM(); toast('❌ '+e.message,true); });
};
function drawM(){
  const c=cur; if(!c) return; const m=me();
  let h='<h3 style="color:var(--p)">💸 Approve ও কমিশন</h3><p class="rfx-s" style="margin:4px 0 10px">Referrer: <b>'+E(c.ref.name)+'</b> ('+E(c.r.referrerId)+')<br>নতুন মেম্বার: '+E(c.r.newStudentName)+' ('+E(c.r.newStudentId)+')</p>'
   +'<div class="rfx-box2" style="font-size:14px">💰 রেফারার পাবেন: <b>৳'+c.amt+'</b></div>'
   +'<div class="rfx-h">👑 আপনার বোনাস (অটোমেটিক, পরিবর্তন হয় না)</div>'
   +'<div class="rfx-row auto"><span>👑</span><div style="flex:1"><b>'+E(m.name)+'</b><br><small>Senior Team Leader</small></div><b>'+SEN_RATE+'%</b><span class="rfx-amt">৳'+comm(c.amt,SEN_RATE)+'</span></div>'
   +'<div class="rfx-h">👔 টিম কমিশন (ঐচ্ছিক — ৩% বা ৫%)</div>';
  if(!c.team.length) h+='<p class="rfx-s" style="margin-bottom:8px">এই রেফারারের কোনো Team Leader / Trainer অ্যাসাইন করা নেই।</p>';
  c.team.forEach((t,i)=>{
    h+='<div class="rfx-row"><input type="checkbox" class="rfx-cb" value="'+i+'"><div style="flex:1"><b>'+E(t.name)+'</b><br><small>'+E(t.category)+'</small></div>'
     +'<select class="rfx-sel" data-i="'+i+'">'+RATES.map(p=>'<option value="'+p+'"'+(p===DEF[t.category]?' selected':'')+'>'+p+'%</option>').join('')+'</select><span class="rfx-amt" id="rfxA'+i+'">৳'+comm(c.amt,DEF[t.category])+'</span></div>';
  });
  /* ===== নতুন: Team Leader / Trainer Assign ===== */
  h+='<div class="rfx-h">👥 Team Leader / Trainer Assign (ঐচ্ছিক)</div><p class="rfx-s" style="margin-bottom:8px">নতুন স্টুডেন্ট <b>'+E(c.r.newStudentName)+'</b> ('+E(c.r.newStudentId)+') কাদের আন্ডারে যাবে টিক দিন:</p>';
  if(!c.all.length) h+='<p class="rfx-s">কোনো Team Leader / Trainer পাওয়া যায়নি।</p>';
  let last='';
  c.all.forEach((t,i)=>{
    if(t.category!==last){ last=t.category; h+='<div class="rfx-s" style="font-weight:700;margin:6px 0 4px">'+(t.category==='Team Leader'?'💼 Team Leader':'🎯 Trainer')+'</div>'; }
    const had=!!c.already[t.key];
    h+='<label class="rfx-row" style="cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" class="rfx-ab" value="'+i+'"'+(had?' checked disabled':'')+'><div style="flex:1"><b>'+E(t.name)+'</b> <small class="rfx-s">('+E(t.loginId)+') • স্টুডেন্ট '+N(t.n)+' জন</small>'+(had?'<br><small style="color:#27ae60">✔ আগে থেকেই এই টিমে আছে</small>':'')+'</div></label>';
  });
  h+='<div class="rfx-box2" id="rfxTot" style="font-size:13px"></div>'
   +'<button class="rfx-btn" style="background:#27ae60" id="rfxYes" onclick="rfxConfirm()">✅ Approve করে টাকা ও কমিশন দিন</button>'
   +'<button class="rfx-btn" style="background:#57606f" onclick="rfxClose()">✖ বাতিল</button>';
  g('rfxBox').innerHTML=h; upd();
}
function upd(){
  const c=cur; if(!c) return; let team=0;
  g('rfxBox').querySelectorAll('.rfx-sel').forEach(s=>{ g('rfxA'+s.dataset.i).textContent='৳'+comm(c.amt,s.value); });
  g('rfxBox').querySelectorAll('.rfx-cb:checked').forEach(cb=>{ const s=g('rfxBox').querySelector('.rfx-sel[data-i="'+cb.value+'"]'); team+=comm(c.amt,s.value); });
  const sb=comm(c.amt,SEN_RATE);
  g('rfxTot').innerHTML='মোট বের হবে: রেফারার ৳'+c.amt+' + টিম ৳'+(Math.round(team*100)/100)+' + আপনার বোনাস ৳'+sb+' = <b>৳'+(Math.round((c.amt+team+sb)*100)/100)+'</b>';
}
g('rfxBox').addEventListener('change',upd);

window.rfxConfirm=function(){
  if(busy||!cur) return; const c=cur, m=me();
  const sel=[...g('rfxBox').querySelectorAll('.rfx-cb:checked')].map(cb=>{
    const t=c.team[+cb.value], rate=N(g('rfxBox').querySelector('.rfx-sel[data-i="'+cb.value+'"]').value);
    return {key:t.key,name:t.name,category:t.category,rate:rate,amount:comm(c.amt,rate)};
  });
  if(sel.some(x=>RATES.indexOf(x.rate)<0||!(x.amount>0))){ toast('❌ কমিশন শুধু ৩% বা ৫% হতে পারে',true); return; }
  const asg=[...g('rfxBox').querySelectorAll('.rfx-ab:checked:not(:disabled)')].map(cb=>{ const t=c.all[+cb.value]; return {key:t.key,name:t.name,category:t.category,loginId:S(t.loginId),phone:S(t.phone)}; });
  if(!confirm('Approve করে '+c.ref.name+'-কে ৳'+c.amt+' দেবেন'+(sel.length?', '+sel.length+' জনকে কমিশন':'')+(asg.length?' এবং নতুন স্টুডেন্টকে '+asg.length+' জনের টিমে অ্যাসাইন করবেন':'')+'?')) return;
  busy=true; g('rfxYes').disabled=true; g('rfxYes').textContent='⏳ প্রসেস হচ্ছে...';
  const now=Date.now();
  bigDb().ref('referrals/'+c.k).transaction(x=>{
    if(x===null) return x;
    if((x.status||'Pending')!=='Pending') return;
    x.status='Approved'; x.reward=c.amt; x.approvedBy={key:m.key,name:m.name,category:m.category}; x.approvedAt=now; return x;
  }).then(res=>{
    if(!res.committed){ busy=false; closeM(); toast('⚠️ এটি আগেই অন্য কেউ প্রক্রিয়া করেছে',true); return; }
    return payAll(c,sel,asg,m,now);
  }).catch(e=>{ busy=false; toast('❌ '+e.message,true); });
};
function payAll(c,sel,asg,m,now){
  const d=new Date(), ds=d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric'}), ts=d.toLocaleTimeString('en-US',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hour12:true});
  const bd=bigDb(), jobs=[], iid='INC-'+Math.random().toString(36).substring(2,10).toUpperCase();
  /* ১) রেফারার */
  jobs.push(bd.ref('users/'+c.ref.key+'/balance').transaction(v=>N(v)+c.amt));
  jobs.push(bd.ref('income_history/'+iid).set({studentId:S(c.r.referrerId),amount:c.amt,category:'Referral Income',description:'Referral bonus for student '+S(c.r.newStudentId),timestamp:now,date:ds,time:ts,incomeRefId:iid,approvedBy:m.name}));
  /* ২) টিম + Senior অটো ৩% */
  const bonus=comm(c.amt,SEN_RATE);
  const all=sel.concat([{key:m.key,name:m.name,category:'Senior Team Leader',rate:SEN_RATE,amount:bonus}]);
  all.forEach(x=>{
    const base=db.ref('subAdmins/'+x.key), t=db.ref('subAdminTransactions').push();
    jobs.push(base.child('balance').transaction(v=>N(v)+x.amount));
    jobs.push(base.child('totalAdded').transaction(v=>N(v)+x.amount));
    jobs.push(t.set({id:t.key,subAdminKey:x.key,type:'Commission',amount:x.amount,description:x.rate+'% referral commission - Referral Income (Referrer '+S(c.r.referrerId)+', ৳'+c.amt+')',date:d.toLocaleString(),timestamp:now}));
  });
  /* ২.৫) নতুন স্টুডেন্টকে TL/Trainer-এর আন্ডারে অ্যাসাইন (একটি আপডেটে) */
  if(asg.length){
    const nu=umap[S(c.r.newStudentId).toUpperCase()]||{}, nsk=sk(c.r.newStudentId), up={};
    asg.forEach(x=>{
      up['subAdmins/'+x.key+'/students/'+nsk]={name:S(nu.name||c.r.newStudentName),studentId:S(c.r.newStudentId),mobile:S(nu.phone),resellerId:S(nu.resellerId),assigned:true,createdAt:now,assignedAt:now};
      up['studentAssignments/'+nsk+'/'+x.key]={key:x.key,name:x.name,category:x.category,loginId:x.loginId,phone:x.phone,assignedAt:now,assignedBy:m.name};
    });
    jobs.push(db.ref().update(up));
    jobs.push(db.ref('subAdminLogs').push({type:'assign',text:'Referral Approve: '+S(c.r.newStudentName)+' ('+S(c.r.newStudentId)+') → '+asg.map(x=>x.name).join(', '),actorKey:m.key,actorName:m.name,actorCategory:m.category,ts:now}));
  }
  /* ৩) রিপোর্টের জন্য লগ */
  const tc=Math.round(sel.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0)*100)/100;
  jobs.push(db.ref('seniorReferralLog').push({action:'Approved',referralKey:c.k,referrerId:S(c.r.referrerId),referrerName:S(c.ref.name),newStudentId:S(c.r.newStudentId),newStudentName:S(c.r.newStudentName),amount:c.amt,
    seniorKey:m.key,seniorName:m.name,commissions:sel,commissionTotal:tc,assignedTo:asg.map(x=>({key:x.key,name:x.name,category:x.category})),seniorBonus:{rate:SEN_RATE,amount:bonus},ts:now}));
  return Promise.all(jobs).then(()=>{ busy=false; closeM(); delete amts[c.k]; toast('✅ Approve হয়েছে, টাকা ও কমিশন দেওয়া হয়েছে'); if(typeof boom==='function') boom(); })
    .catch(e=>{ busy=false; toast('⚠️ কিছু পেমেন্ট সম্পন্ন হয়নি: '+e.message,true); });
}

/* ---------- Reject ---------- */
window.rfxReject=function(k){
  if(!isSen()||busy) return;
  const r=refs.find(x=>x.key===k); if(!r||(r.status||'Pending')!=='Pending'){ toast('⚠️ এটি আগেই প্রক্রিয়া হয়েছে',true); return; }
  const reason=prompt('Reject-এর কারণ (ঐচ্ছিক):'); if(reason===null) return;
  const m=me(), now=Date.now(); busy=true;
  bigDb().ref('referrals/'+k).transaction(x=>{
    if(x===null) return x;
    if((x.status||'Pending')!=='Pending') return;
    x.status='Rejected'; x.rejectedBy={key:m.key,name:m.name,category:m.category}; x.rejectedAt=now; x.rejectReason=S(reason).trim(); return x;
  }).then(res=>{
    busy=false;
    if(!res.committed){ toast('⚠️ এটি আগেই প্রক্রিয়া হয়েছে',true); return; }
    db.ref('seniorReferralLog').push({action:'Rejected',referralKey:k,referrerId:S(r.referrerId),referrerName:nameOf(r.referrerId),newStudentId:S(r.newStudentId),newStudentName:S(r.newStudentName),amount:0,seniorKey:m.key,seniorName:m.name,reason:S(reason).trim(),ts:now});
    toast('❌ Reject করা হয়েছে');
  }).catch(e=>{ busy=false; toast('❌ '+e.message,true); });
};

/* ---------- লিসেনার ---------- */
function stop(){ hs.forEach(r=>{ try{ r.off(); }catch(e){} }); hs=[]; refs=[]; umap={}; staff={}; logs=[]; }
function on(r,f){ hs.push(r); r.on('value',s=>{ try{ f(s); }catch(e){ console.error(e); } },e=>console.error(e)); }
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, ok=!!(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isSen()), s=ok?k:'';
  g('rfxHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!ok);
  if(s===sig) return;
  stop(); sig=s; if(!s) return;
  const bd=bigDb();
  on(bd.ref('referrals'),sn=>{ const a=[]; sn.forEach(c=>{ a.push(Object.assign({key:c.key},c.val())); return false; }); refs=a; render(); });
  on(bd.ref('users'),sn=>{ const m={}; sn.forEach(c=>{ const u=c.val()||{}; if(u.id) m[S(u.id).toUpperCase()]={key:c.key,name:u.name,phone:u.phone,resellerId:u.resellerId}; return false; }); umap=m; render(); });
  on(db.ref('subAdmins'),sn=>{ const m={}; sn.forEach(c=>{ const v=c.val()||{}; m[c.key]={key:c.key,name:v.name,category:v.category,status:v.status,loginId:v.loginId,phone:v.phone,n:Object.keys(v.students||{}).length}; return false; }); staff=m; });
  on(db.ref('seniorReferralLog').orderByKey().limitToLast(500),sn=>{ const a=[]; sn.forEach(c=>{ a.push(Object.assign({key:c.key},c.val())); return false; }); logs=a; render(); });
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('Senior referral add-on error:',e); }
})();
</script>

  • Team Leader / Trainer / Teacher এটা দেখবেই না (হোম বক্স, ☰ মেনু, পেজ — সব বন্ধ)
  • অ্যাডমিন প্যানেলের মতোই `resellers` নোডে সেভ হয় (একই ফিল্ড, একই ডুপ্লিকেট চেক) -->
<style>

body[data-theme="Senior Team Leader"] .rsv-t div{color:var(--p2);border:1px solid var(--p)}

<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id),S=v=>String(v==null?'':v);
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isSen=()=>role()==='Senior Team Leader';

/* ---------- UI ---------- */
g('dashboardView').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
<div id="resellerSaveSection" class="section"><button class="back-btn" onclick="switchSubTab('home')">← ফিরে যান (Back)</button>
 <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">👤</span><div><h3>Reseller Management</h3><p>নতুন Reseller সেভ করুন</p></div></div>
 <div class="card">
  <h3>➕ Add New Reseller</h3>
  <div class="form-row">
   <div class="form-group"><label>Reseller Name *</label><input type="text" id="rsvName" placeholder="Enter reseller name"></div>
   <div class="form-group"><label>Reseller ID (Unique) *</label><input type="text" id="rsvId" placeholder="e.g. RES001"></div>
   <div class="form-group"><label>Phone Number *</label><input type="text" id="rsvPhone" placeholder="Enter phone number" inputmode="numeric"></div>
   <div class="form-group"><label>Student ID (Unique) *</label><input type="text" id="rsvStu" placeholder="Enter student ID"></div>
  </div>
  <button class="btn-primary" id="rsvBtn" onclick="rsvSave()">Save Reseller</button>
 </div>
 <div class="rsv-t" id="rsvSum"></div>
 <div class="card"><div class="form-group" style="margin:0"><input type="text" id="rsvQ" placeholder="🔍 নাম / Reseller ID / Student ID"></div></div>
 <div id="rsvList"></div>
</div>`);
document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
<div id="rsvOv"><div id="rsvBox">
 <h3 style="color:var(--p)">✏️ Reseller Edit</h3>
 <div class="form-group" style="margin-top:10px"><label>Reseller ID (পরিবর্তন করা যাবে না)</label><input type="text" id="rseId" disabled></div>
 <div class="form-group" style="margin-top:10px"><label>Reseller Name *</label><input type="text" id="rseName"></div>
 <div class="form-group" style="margin-top:10px"><label>Phone Number *</label><input type="text" id="rsePhone" inputmode="numeric"></div>
 <div class="form-group" style="margin-top:10px"><label>Student ID (Unique) *</label><input type="text" id="rseStu"></div>
 <button class="rsv-b" style="background:#27ae60" id="rseBtn" onclick="rsvEditSave()">✅ Save Changes</button>
 <button class="rsv-b" style="background:#57606f" onclick="rsvEditClose()">⬅ Cancel</button>
</div></div>`);
g('homeSection').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="dash-box hide" id="rsvHomeBox" onclick="switchSubTab(\'resellerSave\')"><div class="ic">👤</div><p>Reseller Save</p></div>');

/* শুধু Senior ছাড়া কেউ এই পেজে ঢুকতে পারবে না */
const _sw=window.switchSubTab;
window.switchSubTab=function(t,n){ if(t==='resellerSave'&&!isSen()) t='home'; return _sw(t,n); };

/* ☰ মেনুতে অপশন (শুধু Senior) */
const dr=g('mnDr');
if(dr) new MutationObserver(()=>{
  const l=dr.querySelector('.mn-list'); if(!l||l.querySelector('.rsv-mn')||!isSen()) return;
  const out=l.querySelector('.mn-out'); if(!out) return;
  const b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.className='mn-it rsv-mn'; b.innerHTML='<span>👤</span>'; b.appendChild(document.createTextNode('Reseller Save'));
  b.onclick=()=>{ dr.classList.remove('open'); const o=g('mnOv'); if(o) o.style.display='none'; switchSubTab('resellerSave'); };
  l.insertBefore(b,out.previousElementSibling);
}).observe(dr,{childList:true});

/* ---------- ডাটা ---------- */
let list=[], ref=null, sig='', q='', shown=60, busy=false;
g('rsvQ').addEventListener('input',e=>{ q=S(e.target.value).toLowerCase().trim(); shown=60; render(); });
window.rsvMore=function(){ shown+=60; render(); };

function render(){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  g('rsvSum').innerHTML='<div><b>'+list.length+'</b>মোট Reseller</div><div><b>'+list.filter(r=>Date.now()-(+r.timestamp||0)<864e5*7).length+'</b>গত ৭ দিনে নতুন</div>';
  const f=list.filter(r=>!q||(S(r.resellerName)+S(r.resellerId)+S(r.studentId)+S(r.phoneNumber)).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1)
    .sort((a,b)=>(+b.timestamp||0)-(+a.timestamp||0));
  g('rsvList').innerHTML=(f.slice(0,shown).map(r=>'<div class="rsv-c"><b>👤 '+E(r.resellerName)+'</b><br>Reseller ID: <b>'+E(r.resellerId)+'</b><br>Student ID: '+E(r.studentId)+'<br>Phone: '+E(r.phoneNumber)+'<br><span class="rsv-s">Created: '+E(r.createdDate||'N/A')+(r.updatedDate?' • Edited: '+E(r.updatedDate):'')+'</span><br><button class="rsv-ed" data-k="'+E(r._k)+'" onclick="rsvEdit(this.dataset.k)">✏️ Edit</button></div>').join('')||"<p class='empty'>কোনো রিসেলার পাওয়া যায়নি।</p>")
   +(f.length>shown?'<div style="text-align:center"><button class="back-btn" onclick="rsvMore()">আরও দেখুন ('+(f.length-shown)+')</button></div>':'');
}

/* ---------- সেভ ---------- */
window.rsvSave=function(){
  if(!isSen()||busy) return;
  const name=g('rsvName').value.trim(), rid=g('rsvId').value.trim(), phone=g('rsvPhone').value.trim(), sid=g('rsvStu').value.trim();
  if(!name||!rid||!phone||!sid){ toast('❌ সবগুলো ঘর পূরণ করুন',true); return; }
  if(/[.#$\[\]\/]/.test(rid)){ toast('❌ Reseller ID-তে . # $ [ ] / ব্যবহার করা যাবে না',true); return; }
  busy=true; g('rsvBtn').disabled=true;
  const done=()=>{ busy=false; g('rsvBtn').disabled=false; };
  db.ref('resellers').once('value',snap=>{
    let dupStu=false,dupId=false;
    snap.forEach(c=>{ const r=c.val()||{}; if(S(r.studentId)===sid) dupStu=true; if(S(r.resellerId)===rid) dupId=true; return false; });
    if(dupStu){ done(); toast('❌ এই Student ID দিয়ে ইতোমধ্যে একটি Reseller Account আছে',true); return; }
    if(dupId){ done(); toast('❌ এই Reseller ID ইতোমধ্যে ব্যবহার করা হয়েছে',true); return; }
    db.ref('resellers/'+rid).set({resellerName:name,resellerId:rid,phoneNumber:phone,studentId:sid,createdDate:new Date().toLocaleString(),timestamp:Date.now()},err=>{
      done();
      if(err){ toast('❌ '+err.message,true); return; }
      toast('✅ Reseller Saved Successfully');
      ['rsvName','rsvId','rsvPhone','rsvStu'].forEach(i=>g(i).value='');
      if(typeof boom==='function') boom();
    });
  },e=>{ done(); toast('❌ '+e.message,true); });
};

/* ---------- এডিট (Reseller ID লক থাকে, কারণ এটাই ডাটাবেস কী) ---------- */
let editKey=null;
window.rsvEditClose=function(){ g('rsvOv').style.display='none'; editKey=null; };
g('rsvOv').addEventListener('click',e=>{ if(e.target===g('rsvOv')&&!busy) window.rsvEditClose(); });
window.rsvEdit=function(k){
  if(!isSen()) return;
  const r=list.find(x=>x._k===k); if(!r) return;
  editKey=k;
  g('rseId').value=S(r.resellerId); g('rseName').value=S(r.resellerName);
  g('rsePhone').value=S(r.phoneNumber); g('rseStu').value=S(r.studentId);
  g('rsvOv').style.display='flex';
};
window.rsvEditSave=function(){
  if(!isSen()||busy||!editKey) return;
  const name=g('rseName').value.trim(), phone=g('rsePhone').value.trim(), sid=g('rseStu').value.trim();
  if(!name||!phone||!sid){ toast('❌ সবগুলো ঘর পূরণ করুন',true); return; }
  if(list.some(x=>x._k!==editKey&&S(x.studentId)===sid)){ toast('❌ এই Student ID দিয়ে আরেকটি Reseller Account আছে',true); return; }
  busy=true; g('rseBtn').disabled=true;
  db.ref('resellers/'+editKey).update({resellerName:name,phoneNumber:phone,studentId:sid,updatedDate:new Date().toLocaleString(),updatedBy:S(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.name)},err=>{
    busy=false; g('rseBtn').disabled=false;
    if(err){ toast('❌ '+err.message,true); return; }
    toast('✅ Reseller আপডেট হয়েছে'); window.rsvEditClose();
  });
};

/* ---------- লিসেনার + দৃশ্যমানতা ---------- */
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=currentSubAdminKey, ok=!!(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isSen()), s=ok?k:'';
  g('rsvHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!ok);
  if(s===sig) return;
  if(ref){ try{ref.off();}catch(e){} ref=null; }
  sig=s; list=[]; g('rsvList').innerHTML='';
  if(!s) return;
  ref=db.ref('resellers');
  ref.on('value',sn=>{ const a=[]; sn.forEach(c=>{ a.push(Object.assign({_k:c.key},c.val()||{})); return false; }); list=a; render(); },e=>console.error(e));
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('Senior reseller add-on error:',e); }
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== সাব-অ্যাডমিন প্যানেল অ্যাড-অন: 🏅 My Trainers (শুধু Team Leader) =====
  বসানোর নিয়ম: সাব-অ্যাডমিন ফাইলের একদম শেষে, অন্য সব ব্লকের পরে, </body> এর ঠিক আগে পেস্ট করুন।
  • অ্যাডমিন প্যানেলে TL Trainership থেকে যেসব Trainer এই Team Leader-কে দেওয়া হয়েছে তারা এখানে দেখাবে
  • কতজন Trainer আছে, প্রতিজন Trainer-এর কাছে কতজন স্টুডেন্ট আছে সেটাও দেখাবে
  • Team Leader নিজের স্টুডেন্ট Trainer-কে দিতে (কপি) ও ফেরত নিতে পারবে
  • Trainer-এর প্যানেলে ওই স্টুডেন্টরা "Team Leader দিয়েছেন" লেখাসহ দেখাবে -->

<script>
(function(){
try{
const g=id=>document.getElementById(id),S=v=>String(v==null?'':v),N=v=>Number(v)||0;
const E=s=>S(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const K=()=>currentSubAdminKey;
const role=()=>(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.category)||localStorage.getItem('subAdminCat')||'';
const isTL=()=>role()==='Team Leader';
const sk=id=>S(id).replace(/[.#$\[\]\/]/g,'_');
const cnt=o=>Object.keys(o||{}).length;
function waNum(n){ let d=S(n).replace(/\D/g,''); if(!d) return ''; if(d.indexOf('00')===0) d=d.slice(2); else if(d.indexOf('01')===0) d='88'+d; return d; }

/* ---------- UI ---------- */
g('dashboardView').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
<div id="myTrainersSection" class="section"><button class="back-btn" onclick="switchSubTab('home')">← ফিরে যান (Back)</button>
 <div class="sec-hero"><span class="si">🏅</span><div><h3>My Trainers</h3><p>আপনার আন্ডারের Trainer ও তাদের স্টুডেন্ট</p></div></div>
 <div class="mt-t" id="mtSum"></div>
 <div id="mtList"></div>
</div>`);
document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div id="mtOv"><div id="mtBox"></div></div>');
g('homeSection').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="dash-box hide" id="mtHomeBox" onclick="switchSubTab(\'myTrainers\')"><div class="ic">🏅</div><p id="mtHomeLbl">My Trainers</p></div>');

/* শুধু Team Leader ঢুকতে পারবে */
const _sw=window.switchSubTab;
window.switchSubTab=function(t,n){ if(t==='myTrainers'&&!isTL()) t='home'; return _sw(t,n); };

/* ☰ মেনুতে অপশন */
const dr=g('mnDr');
if(dr) new MutationObserver(()=>{
  const l=dr.querySelector('.mn-list'); if(!l||l.querySelector('.mt-mn')||!isTL()) return;
  const out=l.querySelector('.mn-out'); if(!out) return;
  const b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.className='mn-it mt-mn'; b.innerHTML='<span>🏅</span>'; b.appendChild(document.createTextNode('My Trainers'));
  b.onclick=()=>{ dr.classList.remove('open'); const o=g('mnOv'); if(o) o.style.display='none'; switchSubTab('myTrainers'); };
  l.insertBefore(b,out.previousElementSibling);
}).observe(dr,{childList:true});

/* ---------- ডাটা ---------- */
let links={}, tr={}, trRefs={}, tsRef=null, sig='', openL={}, busy=false;
function stop(){
  if(tsRef){ try{tsRef.off();}catch(e){} tsRef=null; }
  Object.keys(trRefs).forEach(k=>{ try{trRefs[k].off();}catch(e){} });
  trRefs={}; links={}; tr={};
}
function start(){
  tsRef=db.ref('teamStructure');
  tsRef.on('value',snap=>{
    const m={}; const me=K();
    snap.forEach(c=>{ const v=c.val()||{}; if(v.tlKey===me) m[c.key]=v; return false; });
    links=m;
    Object.keys(trRefs).forEach(k=>{ if(!m[k]){ try{trRefs[k].off();}catch(e){} delete trRefs[k]; delete tr[k]; } });
    Object.keys(m).forEach(k=>{
      if(trRefs[k]) return;
      const r=db.ref('subAdmins/'+k); trRefs[k]=r;
      r.on('value',s=>{ tr[k]=s.exists()?s.val():null; render(); });
    });
    render();
  },e=>{ g('mtList').innerHTML="<p class='empty' style='color:#c0392b'>❌ লোড হয়নি: "+E(e.message)+"</p>"; });
}

const given=t=>Object.keys((t&&t.students)||{}).filter(k=>(t.students[k]||{}).givenByTL===K());

function render(){
  if(!isTL()) return;
  const ids=Object.keys(links).filter(k=>tr[k]&&tr[k].category==='Trainer').sort((a,b)=>S(tr[a].name).localeCompare(S(tr[b].name)));
  const total=ids.reduce((a,k)=>a+cnt(tr[k].students),0), gv=ids.reduce((a,k)=>a+given(tr[k]).length,0);
  g('mtSum').innerHTML='<div><b>'+ids.length+'</b>🎯 মোট Trainer</div><div><b>'+total+'</b>👥 Trainer-দের স্টুডেন্ট</div><div><b>'+gv+'</b>📤 আমার দেওয়া</div>';
  g('mtHomeLbl').textContent='My Trainers'+(ids.length?' ('+ids.length+')':'');
  g('mtList').innerHTML=ids.length?ids.map(k=>{
    const t=tr[k], n=cnt(t.students), gi=given(t).length, num=waNum(t.phone), op=!!openL[k];
    const list=Object.keys(t.students||{}).map(x=>t.students[x]);
    return '<div class="mt-c"><b>🎯 '+E(t.name)+'</b> <span class="mt-s">('+E(t.loginId)+')</span><span class="mt-b">'+n+' জন</span>'
     +(t.status==='Suspended'||t.status==='Inactive'?'<br><span style="color:#e74c3c;font-weight:700">⏸ বন্ধ আছে</span>':'')
     +'<br><span class="mt-s">📱 '+(t.phone?E(t.phone):'নম্বর নেই')+' • আপনার দেওয়া: '+gi+' জন • মোট স্টুডেন্ট: '+n+' জন</span>'
     +'<div class="mt-btns"><button style="background:#27ae60" data-k="'+E(k)+'" onclick="mtGive(this.dataset.k)">➕ স্টুডেন্ট দিন</button>'
     +'<button style="background:#e67e22" data-k="'+E(k)+'" onclick="mtBack(this.dataset.k)">↩ ফেরত নিন</button>'
     +'<button style="background:#3b5bdb" data-k="'+E(k)+'" onclick="mtTog(this.dataset.k)">👥 '+(op?'লুকান':'স্টুডেন্ট দেখুন')+'</button>'
     +(num?'<a style="background:#25D366" href="https://wa.me/'+num+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">💬 WhatsApp</a>':'')+'</div>'
     +(op?'<div class="mt-l">'+(list.map(s=>'<div><b>'+E(s.name)+'</b> <span class="mt-s">'+E(s.studentId)+' • '+E(s.mobile)+(s.givenByTL===K()?' • 📤 আমার দেওয়া':'')+'</span></div>').join('')||'<span class="mt-s">কোনো স্টুডেন্ট নেই।</span>')+'</div>':'')
     +'</div>';
  }).join(''):"<p class='empty'>এখনো আপনার আন্ডারে কোনো Trainer দেওয়া হয়নি। অ্যাডমিন Trainer যোগ করলে এখানে দেখাবে।</p>";
}
window.mtTog=function(k){ openL[k]=!openL[k]; render(); };

/* ---------- মোডাল ---------- */
function closeM(){ g('mtOv').style.display='none'; g('mtBox').innerHTML=''; }
window.mtClose=closeM;
g('mtOv').addEventListener('click',e=>{ if(e.target===g('mtOv')&&!busy) closeM(); });
window.mtFilter=function(v){
  v=S(v).toLowerCase().trim();
  g('mtBox').querySelectorAll('.mt-row').forEach(r=>{ r.style.display=(!v||r.dataset.s.indexOf(v)>-1)?'':'none'; });
};
window.mtAll=function(){
  const cbs=[...g('mtBox').querySelectorAll('.mt-row')].filter(r=>r.style.display!=='none').map(r=>r.querySelector('input')).filter(c=>!c.disabled);
  const on=cbs.some(c=>!c.checked); cbs.forEach(c=>c.checked=on);
};
function shell(title,sub,rows,btnTxt,btnCol,fn,tk){
  g('mtBox').innerHTML='<h3 style="color:var(--p)">'+title+'</h3><p class="mt-s" style="margin:4px 0 8px">'+sub+'</p>'
   +'<input class="mt-in" placeholder="🔍 নাম / ID / মোবাইল" oninput="mtFilter(this.value)">'
   +'<button type="button" class="mt-sel" onclick="mtAll()">☑ সব বাছাই / বাদ</button><div style="margin-top:8px">'+rows+'</div>'
   +'<button class="mt-bt" style="background:'+btnCol+'" id="mtGo" data-k="'+E(tk)+'" onclick="'+fn+'(this.dataset.k)">'+btnTxt+'</button>'
   +'<button class="mt-bt" style="background:#57606f" onclick="mtClose()">✖ বাতিল</button>';
  g('mtOv').style.display='flex';
}

/* ---------- স্টুডেন্ট দিন ---------- */
window.mtGive=function(tk){
  if(!isTL()||busy) return;
  const t=tr[tk]; if(!t) return;
  const mine=(typeof globalStudentsCache!=='undefined'?globalStudentsCache:[]).filter(s=>S(s.studentId).trim());
  if(!mine.length){ toast('⚠️ আপনার কাছে কোনো স্টুডেন্ট নেই',true); return; }
  const have=t.students||{}, seen={};
  const rows=mine.filter(s=>{ const k=sk(s.studentId); if(seen[k]) return false; seen[k]=1; return true; }).map(s=>{
    const k=sk(s.studentId), h=!!have[k];
    return '<label class="mt-row'+(h?' off':'')+'" data-s="'+E((S(s.name)+S(s.studentId)+S(s.mobile)).toLowerCase())+'"><input type="checkbox" class="mt-cb" value="'+E(k)+'" data-i="'+E(s.studentId)+'"'+(h?' checked disabled':'')+'><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><b>'+E(s.name)+'</b><br><span class="mt-s">'+E(s.studentId)+' • '+E(s.mobile)+(h?' • ✔ আগে থেকেই আছে':'')+'</span></div></label>';
  }).join('');
  shell('➕ '+E(t.name)+'-কে স্টুডেন্ট দিন','আপনার কাছেও স্টুডেন্ট থাকবে, Trainer-এর কাছেও যাবে।',rows,'✅ বাছাই করা স্টুডেন্ট দিন','#27ae60','mtGiveGo',tk);
};
window.mtGiveGo=function(tk){
  if(busy) return; const t=tr[tk]; if(!t) return;
  const mine=(typeof globalStudentsCache!=='undefined'?globalStudentsCache:[]);
  const picked=[...g('mtBox').querySelectorAll('.mt-cb:checked:not(:disabled)')].map(c=>mine.find(s=>sk(s.studentId)===c.value)).filter(Boolean);
  if(!picked.length){ toast('❌ কমপক্ষে একজন স্টুডেন্ট বাছাই করুন',true); return; }
  if(!confirm(picked.length+' জন স্টুডেন্ট '+t.name+'-কে দেবেন?')) return;
  const me=K(), meName=S(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.name), now=Date.now(), up={};
  picked.forEach(s=>{
    const k=sk(s.studentId);
    up['subAdmins/'+tk+'/students/'+k]={name:S(s.name),studentId:S(s.studentId),mobile:S(s.mobile),resellerId:S(s.resellerId),assigned:true,givenByTL:me,createdAt:now,assignedAt:now};
    up['studentAssignments/'+k+'/'+tk]={key:tk,name:S(t.name),category:'Trainer',loginId:S(t.loginId),phone:S(t.phone),assignedAt:now,assignedBy:meName};
  });
  busy=true; g('mtGo').disabled=true;
  db.ref().update(up,err=>{
    busy=false;
    if(err){ g('mtGo').disabled=false; toast('❌ '+err.message,true); return; }
    toast('✅ '+picked.length+' জন স্টুডেন্ট '+t.name+'-কে দেওয়া হয়েছে'); closeM();
    try{ db.ref('subAdminLogs').push({type:'assign',text:'TL → Trainer: '+picked.length+' জন স্টুডেন্ট '+meName+' → '+S(t.name),actorKey:me,actorName:meName,actorCategory:'Team Leader',ts:Date.now()}); }catch(e){}
    if(typeof boom==='function') boom();
  });
};

/* ---------- ফেরত নিন (শুধু নিজের দেওয়া স্টুডেন্ট) ---------- */
window.mtBack=function(tk){
  if(!isTL()||busy) return;
  const t=tr[tk]; if(!t) return;
  const ks=given(t);
  if(!ks.length){ toast('⚠️ আপনার দেওয়া কোনো স্টুডেন্ট এই Trainer-এর কাছে নেই',true); return; }
  const rows=ks.map(k=>{ const s=t.students[k]||{};
    return '<label class="mt-row" data-s="'+E((S(s.name)+S(s.studentId)+S(s.mobile)).toLowerCase())+'"><input type="checkbox" class="mt-cb" value="'+E(k)+'"><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><b>'+E(s.name)+'</b><br><span class="mt-s">'+E(s.studentId)+' • '+E(s.mobile)+'</span></div></label>'; }).join('');
  shell('↩ '+E(t.name)+'-এর কাছ থেকে ফেরত নিন','শুধু আপনার দেওয়া স্টুডেন্ট এখানে দেখাচ্ছে।',rows,'↩ বাছাই করাগুলো ফেরত নিন','#e67e22','mtBackGo',tk);
};
window.mtBackGo=function(tk){
  if(busy) return; const t=tr[tk]; if(!t) return;
  const ks=[...g('mtBox').querySelectorAll('.mt-cb:checked')].map(c=>c.value).filter(k=>(t.students||{})[k]&&t.students[k].givenByTL===K());
  if(!ks.length){ toast('❌ কমপক্ষে একজন স্টুডেন্ট বাছাই করুন',true); return; }
  if(!confirm(ks.length+' জন স্টুডেন্ট '+t.name+'-এর কাছ থেকে ফেরত নেবেন?')) return;
  const up={}; ks.forEach(k=>{ up['subAdmins/'+tk+'/students/'+k]=null; up['studentAssignments/'+k+'/'+tk]=null; });
  busy=true; g('mtGo').disabled=true;
  db.ref().update(up,err=>{
    busy=false;
    if(err){ g('mtGo').disabled=false; toast('❌ '+err.message,true); return; }
    toast('✅ '+ks.length+' জন স্টুডেন্ট ফেরত নেওয়া হয়েছে'); closeM();
    try{ const n=S(currentSubAdminData&&currentSubAdminData.name); db.ref('subAdminLogs').push({type:'unassign',text:'TL ← Trainer: '+ks.length+' জন স্টুডেন্ট ফেরত ('+n+' ← '+S(t.name)+')',actorKey:K(),actorName:n,actorCategory:'Team Leader',ts:Date.now()}); }catch(e){}
  });
};

/* ---------- Trainer-এর প্যানেলে লেবেল: "Team Leader দিয়েছেন" ---------- */
const _rs=window.renderStudents;
if(typeof _rs==='function') window.renderStudents=function(){
  _rs.apply(this,arguments);
  try{
    if(role()!=='Trainer') return;
    document.querySelectorAll('#studentsContainer .student-card').forEach(c=>{
      let id=''; c.querySelectorAll('p').forEach(p=>{ const b=p.querySelector('b'); if(b&&b.textContent.trim()==='ID:') id=p.textContent.replace(b.textContent,'').trim(); });
      const s=globalStudentsCache.find(x=>S(x.studentId)===id);
      if(s&&s.givenByTL) c.querySelectorAll('p').forEach(p=>{ if(p.textContent.indexOf('অ্যাডমিন দিয়েছেন')>-1) p.innerHTML='📌 Team Leader দিয়েছেন'; });
    });
  }catch(e){}
};

/* ---------- লগইন/লগআউটে অটো চালু ---------- */
setInterval(()=>{
  const k=K(), ok=!!(k&&currentSubAdminData&&isTL()), s=ok?k:'';
  g('mtHomeBox').classList.toggle('hide',!ok);
  if(s===sig) return;
  stop(); sig=s; g('mtList').innerHTML='';
  if(s) start();
},700);
}catch(e){ console.error('My Trainers add-on error:',e); }
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>
